# ---- 00_belle_epoque.txt ----
REPLACE:je_belle_epoque = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

	group = je_group_crises

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		has_technology_researched = shift_work
		has_technology_researched = camera
		has_technology_researched = electrical_generation
		country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
	}

	possible = {
		is_at_war = no
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = belle_epoque_sol_trigger
			any_scope_state = {
				filter = {
					is_incorporated = yes
				}
				average_sol >= 15
				percent > 0.5
			}
		}
		capital = {
			average_sol >= 15
		}
		capital = {
			any_scope_building = {
				is_building_type = building_urban_center
				level >= 40
				has_active_production_method = pm_electric_streetlights
			}
			devastation = 0
			turmoil < 0.1
		}

		any_scope_state = {
			any_scope_building = {
				is_building_type = building_art_academy
				level >= 3
				OR = {
				has_active_production_method = pm_photographic_art
					has_active_production_method = pm_film_art
				}
			}
		}

		any_scope_state = {
			any_scope_building = {
				is_building_type = building_university
				level >= 10
				OR = {
				has_active_production_method = pm_philosophy_department
					has_active_production_method = pm_analytical_philosophy_department
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			name = belle_epoque_var
			value = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:USA ?= this
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NEW_YORK
				s:STATE_NEW_YORK ?= {
					any_scope_state = { average_sol >= 15 }
				}
			}
			s:STATE_NEW_YORK ?= {
				random_scope_state = {
					save_scope_as = belle_epoque_state
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				c:USA ?= this
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_CALIFORNIA
				s:STATE_CALIFORNIA ?= {
					any_scope_state = { average_sol >= 15 }
				}
			}
			s:STATE_CALIFORNIA ?= {
				random_scope_state = {
					save_scope_as = belle_epoque_state
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				c:USA ?= this
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NEW_YORK
				s:STATE_NEW_YORK ?= {
					any_scope_state = { average_sol >= 15 }
				}
			}
			s:STATE_NEW_YORK ?= {
				random_scope_state = {
					save_scope_as = belle_epoque_state
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					c:TPG ?= this
					c:CHI ?= this
				}
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_GUANGDONG
				s:STATE_GUANGDONG ?= {
					any_scope_state = { average_sol >= 15 }
				}
			}
			s:STATE_GUANGDONG ?= {
				random_scope_state = {
					save_scope_as = belle_epoque_state
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					c:TPG ?= this
					c:CHI ?= this
				}
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_SUZHOU
				s:STATE_SUZHOU ?= {
					any_scope_state = { average_sol >= 15 }
				}
			}
			s:STATE_SUZHOU ?= {
				random_scope_state = {
					save_scope_as = belle_epoque_state
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					c:TPG ?= this
					c:CHI ?= this
				}
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NANJING
				s:STATE_NANJING ?= {
					any_scope_state = { average_sol >= 15 }
				}
			}
			s:STATE_NANJING ?= {
				random_scope_state = {
					save_scope_as = belle_epoque_state
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			capital = {
				save_scope_as = belle_epoque_state
			}
		}
	}

	goal_add_value = {
		add = 120 #10 years timeout
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			scope:journal_entry = {
				is_goal_complete = yes
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_belle_epoque_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = belle_epoque_var
					}
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = belle_epoque_fail_tt
						any_scope_war = {
							any_scope_front = {
								any_scope_state = {
									this = scope:belle_epoque_state
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = belle_epoque_var
					}
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = belle_epoque_invalid_tt
						scope:belle_epoque_state = {
							NOT = {
								owner = root
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_belle_epoque_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	on_weekly_pulse = {
		events = {
			belle_epoque_events.1 # eiffel tower complete
		}
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		random_events = {
			220 = 0
			40 = belle_epoque_events.2 #Art Noveau
			10 = belle_epoque_events.3 #Leader Joins Symbolists
			10 = belle_epoque_events.4 #Cabaret
			30 = belle_epoque_events.5 #Rabies Vaccine
			10 = belle_epoque_events.6 #Film
			10 = belle_epoque_events.7 #Cinéorama
			10 = belle_epoque_events.8 #Steam Car
			10 = belle_epoque_events.9 #Paris-Rouen
			10 = belle_epoque_events.10 #Noveaux Riches
			10 = belle_epoque_events.11 #Roller-Skating
			10 = belle_epoque_events.12 #Metro
			10 = belle_epoque_events.13 #Colonial Exhibitions
			5000 = belle_epoque_events.16 #Grande Roue de Paris
			10 = belle_epoque_events.17 #Fin de Siècle
			10 = belle_epoque_events.18 #Decadent Movement
			10 = belle_epoque_events.19 #Symbolist Manifesto
			10 = belle_epoque_events.20 #Botanical Gardens
		}
		effect = {
			change_variable = {
				name = belle_epoque_var
				add = 1
			}
			random_list = {
				80 = {

				}
				20 = {
					random_country = {
						limit = {
							any_scope_state = {
								any_neighbouring_state = {
									owner = {
										NOT = {
											this = root
										}
										has_journal_entry = je_belle_epoque
										any_scope_building = {
											has_active_production_method = pm_craftsman_sewing
										}
									}
								}
								has_building = building_railway
							}
						}
						trigger_event = { id = belle_epoque_events.14 weeks = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	current_value = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = belle_epoque_var
			}
			value = var:belle_epoque_var
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = belle_epoque_var
				}
				NOT = { exists = scope:belle_epoque_state }
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_belle_epoque_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	progressbar = yes

	weight = 10
}


# ---- 00_canada_australia.txt ----
# Contextless/global JE shared by the Canadian colonies and their overlord, working toward Confederation.
# Folded from the former per-country je_canada_can (colony perspective) and je_canada_gbr (overlord perspective).
REPLACE:je_canada = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	group = je_group_global_international_situations

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	should_be_involved = {
		OR = {
			# The Canadian colonies
			AND = {
				capital = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_canada
				}
				is_subject = yes
				country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			}
			# Their overlord
			AND = {
				NOT = {
					capital = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_canada
					}
				}
				any_subject_or_below = {
					capital = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_canada
					}
					country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
					count >= 2
				}
			}
		}
	}

	should_show_when_not_involved = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				capital = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_canada
				}
				country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			}
			any_subject_or_below = {
				capital = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_canada
				}
				country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			}
		}
	}

	is_shown_in_lobby = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:anglo_canadian
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:franco_canadian
				}
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_canada
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = {
					capital = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_canada
					}
				}
				any_subject_or_below = {
					capital = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_canada
					}
					count >= 2
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		any_country_in_greater_canada = {
			is_subject = yes
			country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			overlord = {
				any_subject_or_below = {
					capital = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_canada
					}
					count >= 2
				}
			}
		}
	}

	possible = {
		any_country_in_greater_canada = {
			is_subject = yes
			country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			OR = {
				has_technology_researched = nationalism
				overlord = {
					has_technology_researched = nationalism
				}
			}
		}
	}

	scripted_button = je_canada_unite_can_button

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			any_country_in_greater_canada = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = prospective_canadian_unifier
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = owns_all_confederate_canada_tt
					any_state_region_in_canadian_confederation = {
						any_scope_state = {
							is_largest_state_in_region = yes
							scope:prospective_canadian_unifier ?= owner
						}
						count = all
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = only_one_remains_in_canada_tt
					any_country_in_canadian_confederation = {
						count = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				s:STATE_ONTARIO ?= {
					random_scope_state = {
						set_hub_name = {
							type = city
							name = HUB_NAME_STATE_ONTARIO_city_ottawa
						}
					}
				}
			}
			je_canada_cleanup = yes
		}
		effect_all_involved = {
			show_as_tooltip = {
				trigger_event = { id = can_aus.5 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						capital = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_canada
						}
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_ONTARIO
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NEW_BRUNSWICK
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_QUEBEC
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NEWFOUNDLAND
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_BRITISH_COLUMBIA
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_BRITISH_COLUMBIA
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_BRITISH_COLUMBIA
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_ONTARIO
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NORTHWEST_TERRITORIES
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NORTHWEST_TERRITORIES
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NORTHWEST_TERRITORIES
					}
					trigger_event = { id = can_aus.5 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							capital = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_canada
							}
						}
					}
					trigger_event = { id = can_aus.6 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				any_country_in_greater_canada = {
					is_subject = yes
					country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_canada_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	on_weekly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			# Pull in any Canadian colony that qualifies after the situation has activated.
			every_country_in_greater_canada = {
				limit = {
					has_journal_entry = je_canada
					is_subject = yes
					country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
					NOT = {
						is_involved_in_journal_entry = je_canada
					}
				}
				je:je_canada ?= {
					add_involved_country = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}

	weight = 100

	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = no
	should_be_pinned_by_default_involved = yes

	can_revolution_inherit = yes
}

REPLACE:je_australia = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	group = je_group_global_international_situations

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	should_be_involved = {
		OR = {
			# The Australasian colonies
			AND = {
				capital = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_australasia
				}
				is_subject = yes
				country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			}
			# Their overlord
			AND = {
				NOT = {
					capital = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_australasia
					}
				}
				any_subject_or_below = {
					capital = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_australasia
					}
					country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
					count >= 2
				}
			}
		}
	}

	should_show_when_not_involved = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				capital = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_australasia
				}
				country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			}
			any_subject_or_below = {
				capital = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_australasia
				}
				country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			}
		}
	}

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		any_country_in_australasia = {
			is_subject = yes
			country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			overlord = {
				any_subject_or_below = {
					capital = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_australasia
					}
					count >= 2
				}
			}
		}
	}

	possible = {
		any_country_in_australasia = {
			is_subject = yes
			country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			OR = {
				has_technology_researched = nationalism
				overlord = {
					has_technology_researched = nationalism
				}
			}
		}
	}

	scripted_button = je_australia_unite_aus_button

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			any_country_in_australasia = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = prospective_australian_unifier
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = owns_all_australasia_tt
					any_state_region_in_australasia = {
						any_scope_state = {
							is_largest_state_in_region = yes
							scope:prospective_australian_unifier ?= owner
						}
						count = all
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = only_one_remains_in_australasia_tt
					any_country_in_australasia = {
						count = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_australia_cleanup = yes
		}
		effect_all_involved = {
			show_as_tooltip = {
				trigger_event = { id = can_aus.7 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						capital = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_australasia
						}
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NEW_SOUTH_WALES
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NEW_SOUTH_WALES
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NEW_SOUTH_WALES
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NEW_SOUTH_WALES
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_WESTERN_AUSTRALIA
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_WESTERN_AUSTRALIA
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_WESTERN_AUSTRALIA
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NORTH_ISLAND
						owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NORTH_ISLAND
					}
					trigger_event = { id = can_aus.7 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							capital = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_australasia
							}
						}
					}
					trigger_event = { id = can_aus.8 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				any_country_in_australasia = {
					is_subject = yes
					country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_australia_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	on_weekly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			# Pull in any Australasian colony that qualifies after the situation has activated.
			every_country_in_australasia = {
				limit = {
					has_journal_entry = je_australia
					is_subject = yes
					country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
					NOT = {
						is_involved_in_journal_entry = je_australia
					}
				}
				je:je_australia ?= {
					add_involved_country = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}

	weight = 100

	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = no
	should_be_pinned_by_default_involved = yes

	can_revolution_inherit = yes
}


# ---- 00_indian_removal.txt ----
REPLACE:je_indian_removal = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	group = je_group_usa_manifest_destiny

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	is_shown_in_lobby = {
		c:USA = THIS
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		random_events = {
			20 = 0
			2 = native_resettlement.2 # john ross. Fires once because very specific.
			2 = native_resettlement.3 # Preparing for resettlement, repeats.
			1 = native_resettlement.4 # Trail of Tears, requires native_resettlement.3 to have fired at least once.
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_no_colonial_affairs
				country_average_culture_pop_acceptance = { culture = cu:cherokee value >= acceptance_status_4 }
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = indian_removal_fail_tt
					NOT = {
						any_scope_state = {
							any_scope_pop = {
								culture = cu:cherokee
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = indian_removal_end
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							any_scope_pop = {
								culture = cu:cherokee
							}
						}
					}
					trigger_event = { id = native_resettlement.5 }
				}
				else = {
					trigger_event = { id = native_resettlement.6 }
				}
			}
			je_indian_removal_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			NOT = { c:SEQ ?= { is_at_war_with_overlord = yes } }
			OR = {
				NOT = { owns_entire_or_is_contesting_state_region = STATE_FLORIDA }
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						exists = c:SEQ
						exists = c:USA
						c:SEQ ?= { is_subject_of = c:USA }
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = { war_may_restore_country = SEQ }
		}
		effect = {
			je_indian_removal_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	weight = 200

		
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes

	transferable = no
}


# ---- 00_land_reclamation.txt ----
REPLACE:je_land_reclamation = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

	group = je_group_internal_affairs

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	is_shown_in_lobby = {
		OR = {
			owns_entire_state_region = STATE_HOLLAND
			owns_entire_state_region = STATE_HOLLAND
		}
	}

	on_yearly_pulse = {
		random_events = {
			4 = 0
			1 = land_reclamation.1
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			scope:journal_entry = {
				is_goal_complete = yes
			}
		}
		effect = {
			remove_modifier = modifier_reclaiming_land
			show_as_tooltip = {
				add_modifier = { # prestige
					name = land_reclamation_complete
					days = long_modifier_time
				}
			}
			je_land_reclamation_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { owns_entire_or_is_contesting_state_region = STATE_HOLLAND }
				NOT = { owns_entire_or_is_contesting_state_region = STATE_HOLLAND }
			}
		}
		effect = {
			remove_modifier = modifier_reclaiming_land
			remove_variable = dutch_land_reclaimer
			je_land_reclamation_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	current_value = {
		value = global_var:land_reclamation_global
	}

	goal_add_value = { # if you change this, update the thresholds in the event
		add = 10
	}

	progressbar = yes

	status_desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = land_reclamation_no_tech
				trigger = {
					NOT = { has_technology_researched = concrete_dockyards }
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = land_reclamation_tech
				trigger = {
					has_technology_researched = concrete_dockyards
				}
			}
		}
	}

	weight = 10
		
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}

REPLACE:je_trans_siberian_railway = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_industry.dds"

	group = je_group_russia_expansion

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		NOT = { has_global_variable = trans_siberian_done }
		owns_entire_state_region = STATE_MOSCOW
		any_state_in_siberia = {
			percent >= 0.5
			owner = ROOT
		}
	}

	possible = {
		has_technology_researched = railways
		any_state_in_trans_siberian_line = {
			percent >= 0.85
			owner = ROOT
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			any_state_in_trans_siberian_line = {
				has_building = building_railway
				percent = 1
			}
			OR = {
				AND = {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_YAKUTSK
						has_building = building_railway
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_OKHOTSK
						has_building = building_railway
					}
				}
				AND = {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_OUTER_MANCHURIA
						has_building = building_railway
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_OUTER_MANCHURIA
						has_building = building_railway
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			set_global_variable = trans_siberian_done
			trigger_event = { id = major_railways.1 }
			show_as_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_journal_entry = je_tsarpoints_master
					}
					je_tsarpoints_add_med = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	weight = 50
}

# ---- 00_meiji_restoration.txt ----
REPLACE:je_meiji_restoration = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/faction_icons/faction_japan_imperial_house.dds"

	group = je_group_meiji_restoration

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	scripted_button = je_meiji_restoration_proclaim_imperial_restoration
	scripted_button = je_meiji_restoration_decide_shogunate_strategy
	scripted_button = je_meiji_restoration_imperial_marriage

	widget = {
		gui = "gui/journal_entry_widgets/ep2_japan_widgets.gui"
		name = "widget_je_meiji_restoration_emperor"
		container = "custom_widget_container_je_icon"
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "gui/journal_entry_widgets/ep2_japan_widgets.gui"
		name = "widget_je_meiji_restoration_daimyos_list"
		container = "custom_widget_container_3"
	}

	is_shown_in_lobby = {
		c:JAP = THIS
	}

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		c:JAP ?= THIS
		has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
		has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
		NOT = { has_variable = japan_restoration_complete }
	}

	possible = {
		has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
		has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
		NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_isolationism }
	}

	immediate = {
		je_meiji_restoration_update_japanese_emperor = yes
		je_meiji_restoration_update_daimyos = yes
		country_calculate_and_cache_daimyo_loyalties_per_state = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:JAP ?= this # Revolters don't get this
			}
			trigger_event = { id = ep2_meiji.1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_political_movement = {
						is_political_movement_type = movement_meiji_restorationist
					}
				}
			}
			create_political_movement = { type = movement_meiji_restorationist }
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_meiji_restorationist
			}
			save_scope_as = meiji_movement_scope
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			save_scope_as = daimyo_ig
		}
		set_variable = { name = restoration_timer_var value = 0 }
	}

	on_weekly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			country_calculate_and_cache_daimyo_loyalties_per_state = yes # We love good script practises
			je_meiji_restoration_update_japanese_emperor = yes
			je_meiji_restoration_update_daimyos = yes
		}
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = restoration_timer_var
					ruler ?= {
						has_trait = special_character_japanese_emperor
					}
					NOT = {
						any_civil_war = {
							civil_war_progress > 0
						}
					}
					NOT = {
						has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
					}
					ig:ig_landowners ?= {
						leader ?= {
							NOT = {
								var:dynasty_var ?= flag:house_tokugawa
							}
						}
					}
				}
				change_variable = { name = restoration_timer_var add = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { name = restoration_timer_var value = 0 }
			}
		}
		random_events = {
			120 = 0
			5 = ep2_meiji_pulse.1
			5 = ep2_meiji_pulse.3
			5 = ep2_meiji_pulse.4
			5 = ep2_meiji_pulse.5
			5 = ep2_meiji_pulse.6
			5 = ep2_meiji_pulse.7
			25 = ep2_meiji_pulse.9
		}
	}

	map_color = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_home_islands
				OR = {
					AND = {
						c:JAP ?= owner
						state_daimyo_loyalty >= 0.8
					}
					AND = {
						c:EZO ?= owner
						s:STATE_HOKKAIDO.region_state:JAP ?= {
							state_daimyo_loyalty >= 0.8
						}
					}
				}
			}
			value = "restoration_shogunate_green_3"
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_home_islands
				OR = {
					AND = {
						c:JAP ?= owner
						state_daimyo_loyalty >= 0.65
					}
					AND = {
						c:EZO ?= owner
						s:STATE_HOKKAIDO.region_state:JAP ?= {
							state_daimyo_loyalty >= 0.65
						}
					}
				}
			}
			value = "restoration_shogunate_green_2"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_home_islands
				OR = {
					AND = {
						c:JAP ?= owner
						state_daimyo_loyalty >= 0.5
					}
					AND = {
						c:EZO ?= owner
						s:STATE_HOKKAIDO.region_state:JAP ?= {
							state_daimyo_loyalty >= 0.5
						}
					}
				}
			}
			value = "restoration_shogunate_green_1"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_home_islands
				OR = {
					AND = {
						c:JAP ?= owner
						state_daimyo_loyalty < 0.2
					}
					AND = {
						c:EZO ?= owner
						s:STATE_HOKKAIDO.region_state:JAP ?= {
							state_daimyo_loyalty < 0.2
						}
					}
				}
			}
			value = "restoration_imperial_red_3"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_home_islands
				OR = {
					AND = {
						c:JAP ?= owner
						state_daimyo_loyalty < 0.35
					}
					AND = {
						c:EZO ?= owner
						s:STATE_HOKKAIDO.region_state:JAP ?= {
							state_daimyo_loyalty < 0.35
						}
					}
				}
			}
			value = "restoration_imperial_red_2"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_home_islands
				OR = {
					AND = {
						c:JAP ?= owner
						state_daimyo_loyalty < 0.5
					}
					AND = {
						c:EZO ?= owner
						s:STATE_HOKKAIDO.region_state:JAP ?= {
							state_daimyo_loyalty < 0.5
						}
					}
				}
			}
			value = "restoration_imperial_red_1"
		}
	}

	map_tooltip = {
		state_region ?= {
			save_scope_as = daimyo_map_state
		}
		owner ?= {
			random_in_list = {
				variable = daimyos_list
				limit = {
					var:daimyo_var ?= scope:daimyo_map_state
				}
				save_scope_as = daimyo_map_character
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_home_islands
				c:JAP ?= owner
				exists = scope:daimyo_map_character
				state_daimyo_loyalty >= 0.5
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = "restoration_map_shogunate_daimyo"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_home_islands
				c:JAP ?= owner
				exists = scope:daimyo_map_character
				state_daimyo_loyalty < 0.5
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = "restoration_map_imperial_daimyo"
		}
		else_if = { # Special cases
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_KYUSHU
				c:JAP ?= owner
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = "restoration_map_satsuma_ryukyu"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_KYUSHU
				c:RYU ?= owner
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = "restoration_map_ryukyu"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_HOKKAIDO
				c:EZO ?= owner
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = "restoration_map_ezochi"
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_home_islands
				c:JAP ?= owner
				scope:daimyo_map_character ?= {
					OR = {
						has_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo
						has_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo_restoration
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = "restoration_map_shinpan_daimyo"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_home_islands
				c:JAP ?= owner
				scope:daimyo_map_character ?= {
					OR = {
						has_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo
						has_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo_restoration
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = "restoration_map_fudai_daimyo"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_home_islands
				c:JAP ?= owner
				scope:daimyo_map_character ?= {
					OR = {
						has_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo
						has_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo_restoration
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = "restoration_map_tozama_daimyo"
		}
	}

	# Imperial Victory
	outcome = {
		header = je_meiji_restoration_imperial_victory_header
		effect_header = je_meiji_restoration_on_imperial_victory_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_meiji_restoration_imperial_victory_conclusion_desc
		flags = { historical }
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = at_least_six_months_tt
				has_variable = restoration_timer_var
				var:restoration_timer_var >= 6
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = ruler_is_japanese_emperor_tt
				ruler ?= {
					has_trait = special_character_japanese_emperor
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				any_civil_war = {
					civil_war_progress > 0
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
			}
			ig:ig_landowners ?= {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = landowners_leader_is_not_tokugawa_tt
					leader ?= {
						NOT = {
							var:dynasty_var ?= flag:house_tokugawa
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			trigger_event = {
				id = meiji.1
			}
			set_variable = japan_emperor_restored
			set_variable = japan_restoration_complete
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_player = no
					c:EZO ?= {
						is_player = no
						is_subject_of = ROOT
						NOT = {
							has_social_hierarchy = edo_system
						}
					}
				}
				annex = c:EZO
			}
		}
		event_outcome_effect_desc = {
			effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_KANTO
						}
					}
					set_capital = STATE_KANTO

							}

					}

				}

			}
		}

	        effect = {

					}
				}
	        }
	    }
	    event_outcome_effect_desc = {

					}
				}
	        }
	    }
	}

	# Shogunate Victory
	outcome = {
		header = je_meiji_restoration_shogunate_victory_header

REPLACE:je_meiji_imperial_marriage = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	group = je_group_meiji_restoration

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = imperial_marriage_ruler
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = japan_imperial_princess
			}
			save_scope_as = imperial_princess_scope
		}
		ordered_scope_treaty = {
			limit = {
				binds = ROOT
				any_scope_article = {
					OR = {
						has_type = trade_privilege
						has_type = foreign_investment_rights
						has_type = law_commitment
					}
				}
			}
			order_by = {
				value = "ROOT.ai_treaty_value(scope:source_country)"
				multiply = -1
			}
			position = 0
			save_scope_as = relevant_treaty_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:relevant_treaty_scope
			}
			scope:journal_entry ?= {
				set_variable = japan_break_treaty_var
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:journal_entry ?= {
						has_variable = japan_break_treaty_var
					}
				}
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:relevant_treaty_scope
					}
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = has_broken_relevant_treaty_tt
						NOT = {
							any_scope_treaty = {
								scope:relevant_treaty_scope ?= this
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = im_broken_applicable_treaty_tt
						NOT = {
							exists = scope:relevant_treaty_scope
						}
					}
				}
			}
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_closed_borders
			NOT = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_free_trade
			}
			NOT = {
				any_state_in_home_islands = {
					is_treaty_port = yes
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = kobu_gattai_progress_tt
				set_variable = japan_completed_imperial_marriage
			}
			je_meiji_imperial_marriage_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = ruler_does_not_have_imperial_marriage_tt
					ruler = {
						NOT = {
							has_variable = japan_imperial_marriage
						}
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_has_timed_out_tt
					NOT = {
						has_journal_entry = je_meiji_imperial_marriage
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			ruler = {
				remove_variable = japan_imperial_marriage
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = kobu_gattai_no_longer_viable_tt
				remove_variable = kobu_gattai
				set_variable = kogi_yoron
			}
			custom_tooltip = kogi_yoron_forced_tt
			je_meiji_imperial_marriage_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = JOURNAL_ENTRY_TIMEOUT
		effect_header = JOURNAL_ENTRY_ON_TIMEOUT
		flags = { timeout }
		conclusion_desc = JOURNAL_ENTRY_TIMEOUT_CONCLUSION_DESC
		effect = {
			custom_tooltip = je_will_fail_tt
			ruler = {
				remove_variable = japan_imperial_marriage
			}
			remove_variable = kobu_gattai
			set_variable = kogi_yoron
			je_meiji_imperial_marriage_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
				has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_meiji_imperial_marriage_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	timeout = 1825

	weight = 10000
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
	can_revolution_inherit = yes
}

								}
							}
						}
					}
					s:STATE_OREGON = {
						any_scope_state = {
							owner = {
								OR = {
									c:GBR = THIS
									is_subject_of = c:GBR
								}
							}
						}
					}
					s:STATE_OREGON = {
						any_scope_state = {
							owner = {
								OR = {
									c:GBR = THIS
									is_subject_of = c:GBR
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				c:GBR ?= { trigger_event = { id = oregon.2 } }
			}
			else = {
				s:STATE_OREGON = {
					add_homeland = cu:yankee
				}
				s:STATE_OREGON = {
					add_homeland = cu:yankee
				}
				s:STATE_OREGON = {
					add_homeland = cu:yankee
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						s:STATE_BRITISH_COLUMBIA = {
							any_scope_state = {
								owner = {
									OR = {
										c:USA ?= THIS
										is_subject_of = c:USA
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					s:STATE_BRITISH_COLUMBIA = {
						add_homeland = cu:yankee
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = oregon_resolved
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
			je_oregon_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	weight = 100
}

REPLACE:je_conquer_oregon = {

	group = je_group_usa_manifest_destiny

	activation_description = {

	}

		trigger = {

				owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_OREGON

		}

		}
	}

REPLACE:je_great_game_control = {

	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = no
	should_be_pinned_by_default_involved = yes
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/objectives/great_game.dds"

	group = je_group_great_game
	dlc = dlc010
	context = none # so this JE within je_group_great_game doesn't inherit: context = country

	activation_description = {
		custom_tooltip = je_great_game_control_activation_tt
	}

	scripted_progress_bar = great_game_core_progress_bar

	widget = {
		gui = "gui/journal_entry_widgets/great_game_widgets.gui"
		name = "widget_je_great_game_active_je_list"
		container = "custom_widget_container_3"
	}

	should_be_involved = {
		OR = {
			c:GBR ?= this
			c:RUS ?= this
			c:BIC ?= this
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_persia
		}
	}

	should_show_when_not_involved = {
		OR = {
			has_strategic_region_interest_tier = {
				strategic_region = sr:region_central_asia
				value >= 3
			}
			has_strategic_region_interest_tier = {
				strategic_region = sr:region_greater_persia
				value >= 3
			}
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_persia
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = { name = great_game_gbr_progress value = 100 }
		set_global_variable = { name = great_game_rus_progress value = 100 }

		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = britain_scope
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = russia_scope
		}

		calculate_great_game_passive_progress = yes

		scope:journal_entry ?= {
			every_scope_je_involved = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							c:GBR ?= this
							c:RUS ?= this
						}
					}
					set_variable = { name = great_game_no_longer_power_bloc_leader_var value = 0 }
				}
				else = {
					set_variable = { name = great_game_subjected_var value = 0 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	map_color = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					c:GBR ?= this
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_persia
					}
				}
				value = "gg_britain_red_2"
			}
			else = {
				value = "gg_britain_red_1"
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					c:RUS ?= this
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_persia
					}
				}
				value = "gg_russia_green_2"
			}
			else = {
				value = "gg_russia_green_1"
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				owner ?= {
					this.power_bloc ?= {
						c:GBR ?= this.power_bloc_leader
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_persia
					}
				}
				value = "gg_britain_red_3"
			}
			else = {
				value = "gg_britain_red_2"
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				owner ?= {
					this.power_bloc ?= {
						c:RUS ?= this.power_bloc_leader
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_persia
					}
				}
				value = "gg_russia_green_3"
			}
			else = {
				value = "gg_russia_green_2"
			}
		}
	}

	map_color_second = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					owner ?= {
						this.power_bloc ?= {
							c:GBR ?= this.power_bloc_leader
						}
					}
				}
				c:GBR.power_bloc ?= {
					leverage_advantage = { target = prev.owner value > 0 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_persia
					}
				}
				value = "gg_britain_red_3"
			}
			else = {
				value = "gg_britain_red_2"
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					owner ?= {
						this.power_bloc ?= {
							c:RUS ?= this.power_bloc_leader
						}
					}
				}
				c:RUS.power_bloc ?= {
					leverage_advantage = { target = prev.owner value > 0 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_persia
					}
				}
				value = "gg_russia_green_3"
			}
			else = {
				value = "gg_russia_green_2"
			}
		}
	}

	map_tooltip = {
		owner ?= {
			save_scope_as = relevant_country
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				owner ?= c:RUS
			}
			owner ?= {
				custom_tooltip_no_bullet = gg_country_russia_direct_controlled_tt
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				owner ?= c:GBR
			}
			owner ?= {
				custom_tooltip_no_bullet = gg_country_britain_direct_controlled_tt
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				owner ?= {
					this.power_bloc ?= {
						c:RUS ?= this.power_bloc_leader
					}
				}
			}
			owner ?= {
				custom_tooltip_no_bullet = gg_country_russia_controlled_tt
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				owner ?= {
					this.power_bloc ?= {
						c:GBR ?= this.power_bloc_leader
					}
				}
			}
			owner ?= {
				custom_tooltip_no_bullet = gg_country_britain_controlled_tt
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					owner ?= {
						this.power_bloc ?= {
							c:RUS ?= this.power_bloc_leader
						}
					}
				}
				c:RUS.power_bloc ?= {
					leverage_advantage = { target = prev.owner value > 0 }
				}
			}
			owner ?= {
				custom_tooltip_no_bullet = gg_country_russia_leveraged_tt
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					owner ?= {
						this.power_bloc ?= {
							c:GBR ?= this.power_bloc_leader
						}
					}
				}
				c:GBR.power_bloc ?= {
					leverage_advantage = { target = prev.owner value > 0 }
				}
			}
			owner ?= {
				custom_tooltip_no_bullet = gg_country_britain_leveraged_tt
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = gg_state_in_casia_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_greater_persia
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = gg_state_in_persia_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_caucasus_old
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = gg_state_in_caucasus_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = gg_state_in_india_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = gg_state_outside_great_game_tt
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_great_game_control_european_victory
		effect_header = je_great_game_control_on_european_victory
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_great_game_control_european_victory_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = great_game_complete_tt
					any_country_in_caucasus_old = {
						count = all
						OR = {
							this.power_bloc ?= {
								c:GBR ?= this.power_bloc_leader
							}
							this.power_bloc ?= {
								c:RUS ?= this.power_bloc_leader
							}
						}
					}
					any_country_in_central_asia = {
						count = all
						OR = {
							this.power_bloc ?= {
								c:GBR ?= this.power_bloc_leader
							}
							this.power_bloc ?= {
								c:RUS ?= this.power_bloc_leader
							}
						}
					}
					any_country_in_greater_persia = {
						count = all
						OR = {
							this.power_bloc ?= {
								c:GBR ?= this.power_bloc_leader
							}
							this.power_bloc ?= {
								c:RUS ?= this.power_bloc_leader
							}
						}
					}
				}
				c:GBR ?= {
					country_rank < rank_value:great_power
				}
				c:RUS ?= {
					country_rank < rank_value:great_power
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			custom_tooltip = outcome_depends_on_score_tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:journal_entry = { scripted_bar_progress = { name = great_game_core_progress_bar value >= 120 } }
						AND = {
							c:GBR ?= { country_rank < rank_value:great_power }
							c:RUS ?= { country_rank >= rank_value:great_power }
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:RUS
					}
					c:RUS ?= {
						trigger_event = { id = gg_core.1 popup = yes }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:GBR
					}
					c:GBR ?= {
						trigger_event = { id = gg_core.2 popup = yes }
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:journal_entry = { scripted_bar_progress = { name = great_game_core_progress_bar value > 80 } }
					scope:journal_entry = { scripted_bar_progress = { name = great_game_core_progress_bar value < 120 } }
					AND = {
						c:GBR ?= { country_rank >= rank_value:great_power }
						c:RUS ?= { country_rank >= rank_value:great_power }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:RUS
					}
					c:RUS ?= {
						trigger_event = { id = gg_core.3 popup = yes }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:GBR
					}
					c:GBR ?= {
						trigger_event = { id = gg_core.3 popup = yes }
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:journal_entry = { scripted_bar_progress = { name = great_game_core_progress_bar value <= 80 } }
						AND = {
							c:RUS ?= { country_rank < rank_value:great_power }
							c:GBR ?= { country_rank >= rank_value:great_power }
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:RUS
					}
					c:RUS ?= {
						trigger_event = { id = gg_core.2 popup = yes }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:GBR
					}
					c:GBR ?= {
						trigger_event = { id = gg_core.1 popup = yes }
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				c:GBR ?= {
					set_variable = achievement_great_game_no_re
				}
				c:RUS ?= {
					set_variable = achievement_great_game_no_re
				}
			}
			je_great_game_control_cleanup = yes
		}
		event_outcome_desc = {
			first_valid = {
				triggered_desc = {
					trigger = {
						OR = {
							scope:journal_entry = { scripted_bar_progress = { name = great_game_core_progress_bar value >= 120 } }
							AND = {
								c:GBR ?= { country_rank < rank_value:great_power }
								c:RUS ?= { country_rank >= rank_value:great_power }
							}
						}
					}
					desc = great_game_outcome_russia_victory_tt
				}
				triggered_desc = {
					trigger = {
						OR = {
							scope:journal_entry = { scripted_bar_progress = { name = great_game_core_progress_bar value <= 80 } }
							AND = {

								c:GBR ?= { country_rank >= rank_value:great_power }
							}
						}
					}
					desc = great_game_outcome_britain_victory_tt
				}
				triggered_desc = {
					desc = event_effects_gg_core.3_tt
				}
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = {
					exists = c:GBR
				}
				NOT = {
					exists = c:RUS
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_great_game_control_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_great_game_control_native_victory
		effect_header = je_great_game_control_on_native_victory
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_great_game_control_native_victory_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = any_casia_country_recognised_tt
				any_country_in_great_game_region = {
					capital = {

							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia

					country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = europeans_expelled_from_casia_tt

					any_state_in_great_game_region = {
						owner ?= {
							OR = {
								c:GBR ?= this
								c:RUS ?= this
								AND = {
									exists = c:GBR
									is_subject_of = c:GBR
								}
								AND = {
									exists = c:RUS
									is_subject_of = c:RUS
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			every_country_in_great_game_region = {
				limit = {
					capital = {
						OR = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_persia_old
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
						}
					}
					is_country_type = recognized
					country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
				}
				trigger_event = { id = gg_core.4 popup = yes }
			}
			c:GBR ?= {
				post_notification = great_game_loss_to_natives
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_great_game_defeated
					days = very_long_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
			c:RUS ?= {
				post_notification = great_game_loss_to_natives
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_great_game_defeated
					days = very_long_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
			je_great_game_control_cleanup = yes
		}
		event_outcome_desc = {
			first_valid = {
				triggered_desc = {
					desc = event_effects_gg_core.4_tt
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_weekly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			scope:journal_entry ?= {
				update_gg_progressbar_tooltips = yes
			}
		}
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			calculate_great_game_passive_progress = yes
			scope:journal_entry ?= {
				every_scope_je_involved = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								c:GBR ?= this
								c:RUS ?= this
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_in_power_bloc = yes
								is_power_bloc_leader = no
							}
							change_variable = {
								name = great_game_no_longer_power_bloc_leader_var
								add = 1
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_in_power_bloc = yes
								is_power_bloc_leader = yes
							}
							set_variable = {
								name = great_game_no_longer_power_bloc_leader_var
								value = 0
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_subject = yes
							}
							change_variable = {
								name = great_game_subjected_var
								add = 1
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_subject = no
							}
							set_variable = {
								name = great_game_subjected_var
								value = 0
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	weight = 1000
	transferable = yes
}

### Russia

REPLACE:je_chinese_concessions = {
		
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = no
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/objectives/great_game.dds"

	group = je_group_great_game
	dlc = dlc010

	activation_description = {
		custom_tooltip = je_chinese_concessions_activation_tt
		custom_tooltip = je_chinese_concessions_activation_2_tt
		custom_tooltip = je_chinese_concessions_activation_3_tt
	}

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		c:RUS ?= THIS
		is_involved_in_journal_entry = je_great_game_control
	}

	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_chinese_concessions_activation_tt
			c:RUS ?= THIS
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_chinese_concessions_activation_2_tt
			has_technology_researched = railways
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_chinese_concessions_activation_3_tt
			any_scope_state = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_OUTER_MANCHURIA
					state_region = s:STATE_OUTER_MANCHURIA
				}
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				any_scope_state = {
					region = sr:region_northeast_asia
					is_treaty_port = yes
				}
				AND = {
					country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_SHENGJING
					country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_SOUTHERN_MANCHURIA
					country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NORTHERN_MANCHURIA
				}
			}
	        any_state_in_manchuria_old = {
	            filter = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = owned_by_russia_or_chinese_country
						OR = {
							owner = c:RUS
							owner = {
								OR = {
									country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
									country_has_primary_culture = cu:han
								}
							}
						}
					}
	            }
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_railway
					levels_owned_by_country = {
						target = c:RUS
						value  >= 1
					}
				}
	            percent >= 1
	        }
		}
		effect = {
			set_global_variable = manchurian_railway_variable
			great_game_russia_progress_15 = yes
			je_chinese_concessions_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				is_involved_in_journal_entry = je_great_game_control
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_chinese_concessions_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	weight = 1000
}

REPLACE:je_maintain_afghan_protectorate = {
		
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = no
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/objectives/great_game.dds"

	group = je_group_great_game
	dlc = dlc010

	activation_description = {
		custom_tooltip = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_activation_tt
		custom_tooltip = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_activation_2_tt
		custom_tooltip = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_activation_3_tt
	}

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		OR = {
			c:GBR ?= THIS
			c:RUS ?= THIS
		}
		is_involved_in_journal_entry = je_great_game_control
	}

	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_activation_tt
			OR = {
				c:GBR ?= THIS
				c:RUS ?= THIS
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_activation_2_tt
			has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_greater_persia
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_activation_3_tt
			NOT = {
				has_global_variable = gg_afghanistan_protectorate_done
			}
		}
		any_country_in_afghanistan = {
			any_neighbouring_state = {
				owner.power_bloc ?= {
					OR = {
						c:GBR.power_bloc ?= THIS
						c:RUS.power_bloc ?= THIS
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			name = afghanistan_variable
			value = 0
		}
		set_global_variable = afghanistan_conflict_started
		cu:pashtun = {
			save_scope_as = pashtun_scope
		}
		cu:tajik = {
			save_scope_as = tajik_scope
		}
		sr:region_greater_persia = {
			save_scope_as = persia_region
		}
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = all_pashtun_or_tajik_countries_puppeted_tt
						any_country_in_greater_persia = {
							filter = {
								OR = {
									country_has_primary_culture = cu:tajik
									country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
								}
							}
							is_subject_of_and_not_being_freed = root
							count = all
						}
					}
				}
				change_variable = { name = afghanistan_variable add = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						any_country_in_greater_persia = {
							OR = {
								country_has_primary_culture = cu:tajik
								country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
							}
						}
					}
					any_state_in_greater_persia = {
						filter = {
							state_region = {
								is_homeland = cu:pashtun
							}
						}
						OR = {
							owner = ROOT
							owner = {
								is_subject_of_and_not_being_freed = ROOT
							}
						}
						percent >= 0.6
					}
				}
				change_variable = { name = afghanistan_variable add = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				change_variable = { name = afghanistan_variable subtract = 1 }
			}
			clamp_variable = { name = afghanistan_variable max = 120 min = 0 }
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					any_country_in_greater_persia = {
						OR = {
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:tajik
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
						}
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = all_pashtun_or_tajik_countries_puppeted_tt
					any_country_in_greater_persia = {
						filter = {
							OR = {
								country_has_primary_culture = cu:tajik
								country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
							}
						}
						is_subject_of_and_not_being_freed = root
						percent = 1
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_else = {
				any_state_in_greater_persia = {
					filter = {
						state_region = {
							is_homeland = cu:pashtun
						}
					}
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = owned_by_this_country_or_subjects_tt
						OR = {
							owner = ROOT
							owner = {
								is_subject_of_and_not_being_freed = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
					percent >= 0.6
				}
			}
			country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KABUL
			country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_HERAT
			country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KABUL
			scope:journal_entry = {
				is_goal_complete = yes
			}
		}
		effect = {
			set_global_variable = gg_afghanistan_protectorate_done
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:RUS ?= this
				}
				set_global_variable = afghanistan_russia_victory_var
				great_game_russia_progress_25 = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					c:GBR ?= this
				}
				set_global_variable = afghanistan_britain_victory_var
				great_game_britain_progress_25 = yes
			}
			je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			any_country_in_greater_persia = {
				filter = {
					OR = {
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:tajik
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
					}
				}
				is_country_type = recognized
				percent = 1
			}
		}
		effect = {
			set_global_variable = gg_afghanistan_protectorate_done
			set_global_variable = afghanistan_afghan_victory_var
			je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = {
					is_involved_in_journal_entry = je_great_game_control
				}
				has_global_variable = gg_afghanistan_protectorate_done
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	current_value = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = afghanistan_variable
			}
			value = root.var:afghanistan_variable
		}
	}

	goal_add_value = {
		add = 120
	}

	progressbar = yes

	is_progressing = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				any_country_in_greater_persia = {
					OR = {
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:tajik
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
					}
				}
			}
			any_country_in_greater_persia = {
				filter = {
					OR = {
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:tajik
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
					}
				}
				is_subject_of_and_not_being_freed = root
				count = all
			}
		}
		trigger_else = {
			any_state_in_greater_persia = {
				filter = {
					state_region = {
						is_homeland = cu:pashtun
					}
				}
				OR = {
					owner = ROOT
					owner = {
						is_subject_of_and_not_being_freed = ROOT
					}
				}
				percent >= 0.6
			}
		}
	}


	weight = 1000
}

REPLACE:je_secure_influence_over_persia = {
		
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = no
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/objectives/great_game.dds"

	group = je_group_great_game
	dlc = dlc010

	activation_description = {
		custom_tooltip = je_secure_influence_over_persia_activation_tt
		custom_tooltip = je_secure_influence_over_persia_activation_2_tt
	}

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		OR = {
			c:GBR ?= THIS
			c:RUS ?= THIS
		}
		is_involved_in_journal_entry = je_great_game_control
	}

	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_secure_influence_over_persia_activation_tt
			OR = {
				c:GBR ?= THIS
				c:RUS ?= THIS
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_secure_influence_over_persia_activation_2_tt
			has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_greater_persia
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			name = persia_variable
			value = 0
		}
		set_global_variable = persia_conflict_started
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:PER
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = persia_puppeted_tt
					c:PER ?= {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_else = {
				any_state_in_greater_persia = {
					filter = {
						state_region = {
							is_homeland = cu:persian
						}
					}
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = owned_by_this_country_or_subjects_tt
						OR = {
							owner = ROOT
							owner = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
					percent >= 0.6
				}
			}
			country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_IRAKAJEMI
			country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_TABRIZ
			country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_LARISTAN
			OR = {
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_KHUZESTAN
				}
				any_subject_or_below = {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_KHUZESTAN
					}
				}
			}
			scope:journal_entry = {
				is_goal_complete = yes
			}
		}
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:RUS ?= this
				}
				set_global_variable = persia_russia_victory_var
				great_game_russia_progress_25 = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					c:GBR ?= this
				}
				set_global_variable = persia_britain_victory_var
				great_game_britain_progress_25 = yes
			}
			je_secure_influence_over_persia_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	current_value = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = persia_variable
			}
			value = root.var:persia_variable
		}
	}

	goal_add_value = {
		add = 120
	}

	progressbar = yes

	is_progressing = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:PER
			}
			c:PER ?= {
				is_subject_of = root
			}
		}
		trigger_else = {
			any_state_in_greater_persia = {
				filter = {
					state_region = {
						is_homeland = cu:persian
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = owned_by_this_country_or_subjects_tt
					OR = {
						owner = ROOT
						owner = {
							is_subject_of = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				percent >= 0.6
			}
		}
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:PER
					c:PER ?= {
						is_subject_of = root
					}
				}
				change_variable = { name = persia_variable add = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT ={
						exists = c:PER
					}
					any_state_in_greater_persia = {
						filter = {
							state_region = {
								is_homeland = cu:persian
							}
						}
						custom_tooltip = {
							text = owned_by_this_country_or_subjects_tt
							OR = {
								owner = ROOT
								owner = {
									is_subject_of = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						percent >= 0.6
					}
				}
				change_variable = { name = persia_variable add = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				change_variable = { name = persia_variable subtract = 1 }
			}
			clamp_variable = { name = persia_variable max = 120 min = 0 }
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
	        c:PER ?= {
	            is_country_type = recognized
	        }
		}
		effect = {
			set_global_variable = persia_persian_victory_var
			je_secure_influence_over_persia_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				is_involved_in_journal_entry = je_great_game_control
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_secure_influence_over_persia_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	weight = 1000
}

# ---- 00_veiled_protectorate.txt ----
REPLACE:je_veiled_protectorate = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	group = je_group_foreign_affairs

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:EGY
			}
			c:EGY ?= {
				is_subject = no
				is_country_type = unrecognized
			}
		}
		trigger_else = {
			always = no
		}
		country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
		any_scope_state = {
			OR = {
				state_region = s:STATE_LOWER_EGYPT
				state_region = s:STATE_UPPER_EGYPT
				state_region = s:STATE_UPPER_EGYPT
				state_region = s:STATE_SINAI
			}
		}
		any_neighbouring_state = {
			owner = c:EGY
		}
	}

	possible = {
		exists = c:EGY
		c:EGY ?= {
			OR = {
				owes_obligation_to = ROOT
				relations:root <= relations_threshold:poor
			}
			country_rank < rank_value:unrecognized_major_power
		}
		NOT = { c:TUR ?= THIS }
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			c:EGY ?= {
				is_subject_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		effect = {
			trigger_event = {
				id = veiled_protectorate.1
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { exists = c:EGY }
				c:EGY ?= {
					is_subject = yes
					NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
				}
				c:EGY ?= {
					NOT = { is_country_type = unrecognized }
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_veiled_protectorate_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = JOURNAL_ENTRY_TIMEOUT
		effect_header = JOURNAL_ENTRY_ON_TIMEOUT
		flags = { timeout }
		conclusion_desc = JOURNAL_ENTRY_TIMEOUT_CONCLUSION_DESC
		effect = {
			je_veiled_protectorate_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	timeout = 3650

	weight = 10000
		
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}

# Simba wa Zanzibar
REPLACE:je_lion_of_the_zanj = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"

	group = je_group_historical_content

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		s:STATE_KENYA = {
			save_scope_as = rift_valley_region
		}
		s:STATE_TANGANYIKA = {
			save_scope_as = tanganyika_region
		}
		s:STATE_KENYA = {
			save_scope_as = kenya_region
		}
		s:STATE_ZANZIBAR = {
			save_scope_as = zanzibar_region
		}
		s:STATE_LINDI = {
			save_scope_as = lindi_region
		}

		cu:swahili = {
			save_scope_as = swahili_scope
		}

		cu:bedouin = {
			save_scope_as = omani_scope
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_country_type = recognized
				AND = {
					owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KENYA
					owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_TANGANYIKA
					owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KENYA
					owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_ZANZIBAR
					owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_LINDI
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = zanzibar.4 }

			custom_tooltip = {
				text = je_lion_of_the_zanj_claims

				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KENYA } }
					s:STATE_KENYA = {
						add_claim = ROOT
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_TANGANYIKA } }
					s:STATE_TANGANYIKA = {
						add_claim = ROOT
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KENYA } }
					s:STATE_KENYA = {
						add_claim = ROOT
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_ZANZIBAR } }
					s:STATE_ZANZIBAR = {
						add_claim = ROOT
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_LINDI } }
					s:STATE_LINDI = {
						add_claim = ROOT
					}
				}
			}


			if = {
				limit = {
					any_primary_culture = { cu:bedouin = this }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_lion_of_the_zanj_homelands_bedouin
					if = {
						limit = {
							owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KENYA
							owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_TANGANYIKA
							owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KENYA
							owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_ZANZIBAR
							owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_LINDI
						}
						s:STATE_ZANZIBAR = {
							add_homeland = cu:bedouin
						}
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					any_primary_culture = { cu:swahili = this }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_lion_of_the_zanj_homelands_swahili
					if = {
						limit = {
							owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KENYA
							owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_TANGANYIKA
							owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KENYA
							owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_ZANZIBAR
							owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_LINDI
						}
						s:STATE_KENYA = {
							add_homeland = cu:swahili
						}
						s:STATE_TANGANYIKA = {
							add_homeland = cu:swahili
						}
						s:STATE_LINDI = {
							add_homeland = cu:swahili
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Fails if Zanzibar is a subject, or if a non-African country manages to establish a foothold in Zanzibar's quote-unquote core territories (roughly Kenya & Tanzania)
	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
	    	OR = {
	    		is_subject = yes
	    		custom_tooltip = {
	    			text = je_lion_of_the_zanj_fail

			        any_neighbouring_state = {
						OR = {
							state_region = s:STATE_KENYA
							state_region = s:STATE_KENYA
							state_region = s:STATE_ZANZIBAR
							state_region = s:STATE_TANGANYIKA
							state_region = s:STATE_LINDI
						}
						OR = {
			    			owner = {
			    				is_not_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_africa
			    				NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT }
			    			}
			    			owner = {
			    				is_subject = yes
			    				top_overlord = {
			    					is_not_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_africa
			    					NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT }
			    				}
			    			}
						}
			    	}
			    }
	        }
		}
		effect = {

			show_as_tooltip = {
				trigger_event = { id = zanzibar.3 } # it's actually triggered by event zanzibar.2
			}

			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					is_capital = no
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_lion_of_the_zanj_loss_of_territory
				}
			}

			hidden_effect = {

				# Collapse of Zanzibar event triggers for Zanzibar's top liege if subject failstate is triggered
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_subject = yes
					}
					top_overlord = { trigger_event = { id = zanzibar.2 } }
				}

				# Collapse of Zanzibar event triggers for colonizer that successfully intruded on Zanzibar's 'natural sphere' if territorial integrity failstate is triggered
				else_if = {
					limit = {
				        any_neighbouring_state = {
							OR = {
								state_region = s:STATE_KENYA
								state_region = s:STATE_KENYA
								state_region = s:STATE_ZANZIBAR
								state_region = s:STATE_TANGANYIKA
								state_region = s:STATE_LINDI
							}
							OR = {
								owner = {
				    				is_not_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_africa
				    			}
				    			owner = {
				    				is_subject = yes
				    				top_overlord = {
				    					is_not_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_africa
				    				}
				    			}
							}
						}
		    		}

			    	random_neighbouring_state = {
			    		limit = {
							OR = {
								state_region = s:STATE_KENYA
								state_region = s:STATE_KENYA
								state_region = s:STATE_ZANZIBAR
								state_region = s:STATE_TANGANYIKA
								state_region = s:STATE_LINDI
							}
							OR = {
								owner = {
				    				is_not_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_africa
				    			}
				    			owner = {
				    				is_subject = yes
				    				top_overlord = {
				    					is_not_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_africa
				    				}
				    			}
							}
			    		}
	    				if = {
	    					limit = {
	    						owner = { is_not_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_africa }
	    					}
	    					owner = { trigger_event = { id = zanzibar.2 } }
	    				}
	    				else_if = {
	    					limit = {
	    						owner = {
	    							NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT }
			        				AND = {
				        				is_subject = yes
				        				top_overlord = {
				        					is_not_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_africa
				        					NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT }
				        				}
			        				}
			        			}
	    					}
	    					owner = {
	    						top_overlord = { trigger_event = { id = zanzibar.2 } }
	    					}
	    				}
			    	}
			    }
		    }
			je_lion_of_the_zanj_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			c:ZAN ?= {
				is_not_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_africa
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_lion_of_the_zanj_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	on_yearly_pulse = {
		random_events = {
			chance_of_no_event = 80

			20 = zanzibar.5 # tippu tip
		}
	}

	weight = 10

		
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}

	}

	}

		trigger = {
			country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_ORAN
			country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_ALGIERS
			country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_CONSTANTINE
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:TUG
				}
				c:TUG ?= {
					OR = {
						is_country_alive = no
						is_subject_of = root
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			custom_tooltip = je_conquest_of_algeria_tt
			trigger_event = algeria_events.1
			show_as_tooltip = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
							state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
							state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
						}
					}
					add_modifier = {
						name = crushed_algerian_resistance
						days = very_long_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_or_is_contesting_state_in_state_region = STATE_ORAN }
			NOT = { has_or_is_contesting_state_in_state_region = STATE_ALGIERS }
			NOT = { has_or_is_contesting_state_in_state_region = STATE_CONSTANTINE }
		}
		effect = {
			trigger_event = algeria_events.3
		}
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		random_events = {
			90 = 0
			10 = algeria_events.5 # Raid Event
		}
	}

	transferable = no


	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes

	weight = 5000
}

REPLACE:je_the_algerian_departements = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	group = je_group_france_expansion
	dlc = dlc004

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {

			}
			save_scope_as = je_algiers_state_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
			}
			save_scope_as = je_oran_state_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
			}
			save_scope_as = je_constantine_state_scope
		}
		cu:berber = {
			save_scope_as = berber_culture_scope
		}
		cu:maghrebi = {
			save_scope_as = maghrebi_culture_scope
		}
		cu:french = {
			save_scope_as = french_culture_scope
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
					state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
					state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = crushed_algerian_resistance
				days = very_long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = je_the_algerian_departements_tt_1
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
					is_incorporated = yes
					is_split_state = no
					turmoil < 0.1
				}
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
					is_incorporated = yes
					is_split_state = no
					turmoil < 0.1
				}
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
					is_incorporated = yes
					is_split_state = no
					turmoil < 0.1
				}
			}
			OR = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_the_algerian_departements_tt_3
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_ALGIERS
						culture_percent_state = {
							target = cu:french
						    value >= 0.2
						}
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_ORAN
						culture_percent_state = {
							target = cu:french
						    value >= 0.2
						}
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_CONSTANTINE
						culture_percent_state = {
							target = cu:french
						    value >= 0.2
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					country_average_religious_acceptance = { target = rel:sunni value >= religious_acceptance_medium }
					country_average_culture_pop_acceptance = { culture = cu:maghrebi value >= acceptance_status_3 }
					country_average_culture_pop_acceptance = { culture = cu:berber value >= acceptance_status_3 }
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			trigger_event = algeria_events.4
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_or_is_contesting_state_in_state_region = STATE_ORAN }
			NOT = { has_or_is_contesting_state_in_state_region = STATE_ALGIERS }

REPLACE:je_confederation_of_the_rhine = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	group = je_group_france_expansion

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		OR = {
			any_subject_or_below = {
				capital = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_german_confederation
				}
			}
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = {
					OR = {
						is_homeland = cu:south_german
						is_homeland = cu:north_german
					}
				}
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_german_confederation
				is_incorporated = no
				NOT = {
					ROOT = {
						has_claim = prev.state_region
					}
				}
			}
		}
		NOR = {
			country_has_primary_culture = cu:north_german
			country_has_primary_culture = cu:south_german
		}
	}

	possible = {
		country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
		any_state_in_german_confederation = {
			OR = {
				owner = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					NOT = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = ROOT
							type = personal_union
							is_initiator = no
						}
					}
					capital = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_german_confederation
					}
					OR = {
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:north_german
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:south_german
					}
				}
				owner = ROOT
			}
			count >= 4
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = rhine_overlord
		cu:north_german = {
			save_scope_as = north_german_scope
		}
		cu:south_german = {
			save_scope_as = south_german_scope
		}
	}

	scripted_button = je_confederation_of_the_rhine_confederate_button
	scripted_button = je_confederation_of_the_rhine_expand_button

			custom_tooltip = {
				text = no_german_great_powers_tt

					any_country_in_german_confederation = {
						OR = {
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:north_german
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:south_german
						}
						country_rank = rank_value:great_power
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_confederation_of_the_rhine_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			exists = c:RHN
			NOT = { c:RHN ?= { is_at_war_with_overlord = yes } }
			c:RHN ?= {
				NOT = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_fall_of_germany
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
			je_confederation_of_the_rhine_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {

				}

REPLACE:je_paraguayan_war = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

        }
    }

    outcome = {
        header = je_standard_completion_header
