        conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc

            OR = {
                AND = {
                    custom_tooltip = {
                        text = je_paraguayan_war_tt_1

                                has_variable = recently_lost_war
                            }
                        }
                        has_variable = recently_won_war
                    }
                    c:PRG ?= {
                        NOT = {
                            has_state_in_state_region_uncontested = STATE_MATO_GROSSO
                            has_state_in_state_region_uncontested = STATE_SANTA_FE
                            has_state_in_state_region_uncontested = STATE_CHACO
                        }
                    }
                }
                AND = {

                        text = je_paraguayan_war_tt_1
                        has_variable = recently_won_war

                            is_country_alive = yes
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        effect = {
            trigger_event = { id = paraguay.7 popup = yes }
        }
    }

                has_variable = recently_lost_war

                has_state_in_state_region_uncontested = STATE_SANTA_FE
                OR = {
                    country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_SANTA_FE
                    country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_SANTA_FE
                    country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_SAO_PAULO
                    country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_RIO_GRANDE_DO_SUL
                }
            }
        }
        effect = {
            trigger_event = { id = paraguay.10 popup = yes }
        }
    }

    outcome = {
        flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
        conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc

            c:PRG ?= {
                has_journal_entry = je_expanding_paraguay

                    has_war_with = root

                    is_war = yes

# ---- 02_pedro_brazil.txt ----
REPLACE:je_pedro_brazil = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/law_icons/monarchy.dds"

	group = je_group_brazil_pedro
	dlc = dlc008

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	is_shown_in_lobby = {
		has_dlc_feature = rp1_content #Colossus of the South
		c:BRZ = THIS
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		events = {
			# Landowners become republicans
			pedro.2
			# AF/LA are marginalized
			pedro.5
			# AF/LA are powerful and not in government
			pedro.6
			# Republicans ask Pedro to be President
			pedro.9
			# Pedro signs the constitution
			pedro.11
		}
		random_events = {
			100 = 0
			10 = pedro.4 # Pedro becomes ill
			10 = pedro.12 # Death of Pedro's heirs
			10 = pedro.14 # To the Highest Standards
		}
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_civil_war = {
						civil_war_progress > 0.5
					}
					NOT = { has_variable = je_pedro_civilwar_point }
				}
				change_variable = { name = pedropoints subtract = 1 }
				set_variable = { name = je_pedro_civilwar_point years = 3 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						in_default = yes
						has_modifier = declared_bankruptcy
					}
					NOT = { has_variable = je_pedro_default_point }
				}
				change_variable = { name = pedropoints subtract = 1 }
				set_variable = { name = je_pedro_default_point }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
					NOT = { has_variable = je_pedro_gp_point }
				}
				change_variable = { name = pedropoints add = 1 }
				set_variable = { name = je_pedro_gp_point }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			name = pedropoints
			value = 0
		}
		root = {
			save_scope_as = brazil_scope
		}
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			save_scope_as = intelligentsia_ig
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		conclusion_desc = je_pedro_success
		color = "gold_positive"
		trigger = {
			country_has_monarchy_law = yes
			AND = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_pedro_success
					var:pedropoints > 5
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = pedro_dead
					ruler = {
						NOT = {
							has_variable = is_pedro
						}
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = {
					has_variable = kingdom_var
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				ruler ?= { set_character_immortal = no }
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = heir
					}
					heir ?= { set_character_immortal = no }
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pedro_heir_empowered_tt
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pedro_je_royalists_more_common_tt
				set_variable = brz_enduring_empire
			}
			# Monarchy survives
			trigger_event = {
				id = pedro.7
				popup = yes
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = je_pedro_fail
						var:pedropoints <= 0
					}
					ig:ig_landowners ?= {
						ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					}
					ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
						ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					}
				}
				AND = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = je_pedro_fail_2
						var:pedropoints <= 5
					}
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = pedro_dead
						ruler = {
							NOT = {
								has_variable = is_pedro
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = {
					has_variable = kingdom_var
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				ruler ?= { set_character_immortal = no }
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = heir
					}
					heir ?= { set_character_immortal = no }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_RIO_GRANDE_DO_SUL
						}
					}
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = { state_region = s:STATE_RIO_GRANDE_DO_SUL }
				        set_hub_name = {
				            type = port
				            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_RIO_GRANDE_DO_SUL_port_bra_republic
				        }
			        }
				}
			}
			# Monarchy ended by coup
			trigger_event = {
				id = pedro.8
				popup = yes
			}
			show_as_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_landed_voting }
					}
					activate_law = law_type:law_landed_voting
				}
				activate_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic
				if = {
					limit = {
						ig:ig_landowners ?= {
							is_in_government = no
							ig_counts_as_marginal = no
						}
					}
					ig:ig_landowners ?= {
						add_ruling_interest_group = yes
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
							is_in_government = no
							ig_counts_as_marginal = no
						}
					}
					ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
						add_ruling_interest_group = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOR = {
					country_has_monarchy_law = yes
					has_variable = ruler_selector_var
				}
				has_variable = kingdom_var
			}
		}
		effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				ruler ?= { set_character_immortal = no }
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = heir
					}
					heir ?= { set_character_immortal = no }
				}
			}
			je_pedro_brazil_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	current_value = {
		value = root.var:pedropoints
	}

	goal_add_value = {
		add = 10
	}

	progressbar = yes

	weight = 100

	can_revolution_inherit = no
	transferable = no
		
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}

REPLACE:je_pedro_republic = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/law_icons/presidential_republic.dds"

	dlc = dlc008

		always = yes
	}

		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_presidential_republic
		}
		effect = {
			# the actual ending event needs to be triggered from on_law_enactment_pass
			show_as_tooltip = {
				ruler = {
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
			je_pedro_republic_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_pedro_brazil }
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = pedro_dead
					ruler = {
						NOT = {
							has_variable = is_pedro
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_pedro_republic_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				country_has_monarchy_law = yes
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_presidential_republic
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_pedro_republic_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = JOURNAL_ENTRY_TIMEOUT
		effect_header = JOURNAL_ENTRY_ON_TIMEOUT
		flags = { timeout }
		conclusion_desc = JOURNAL_ENTRY_TIMEOUT_CONCLUSION_DESC
		effect = {
			remove_pedropoint_effect = yes
			je_pedro_republic_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	timeout = 3600

	weight = 100
	can_revolution_inherit = no
	transferable = no

	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}

		}
	}

		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"

				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_no_womens_rights
			}

		}
		effect = {

				id = pedro.13

				text = isabel_je_tt

			show_as_tooltip = {
				ruler ?= { remove_modifier = isabel_heir_popularity }
				heir ?= { remove_modifier = isabel_heir_popularity }
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_pedro_brazil }
				heir ?= {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = is_isabella_tt
						NOT = {
							has_variable = isabel_var
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					exists = heir
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_isabel_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_no_womens_rights
					}
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = isabel_points
				add = 1
			}
		}
	}

	is_progressing = {
		has_journal_entry = je_pedro_brazil
		scope:target ?= {
			is_heir_of_own_country = yes
		}
		NOT = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_no_womens_rights
		}
	}

	current_value = {
		value = root.var:isabel_points
	}

	goal_add_value = {
		add = 120
	}

	progressbar = yes

	weight = 100
	can_revolution_inherit = no
	transferable = no

}

	scripted_button = je_consolidate_afghanistan_north_status_quo
	scripted_button = je_consolidate_afghanistan_offer_compensation
	scripted_button = je_consolidate_afghanistan_threaten_reprisals
	scripted_button = je_consolidate_afghanistan_approve_of_treaty
	scripted_button = je_consolidate_afghanistan_retract_treaty
	scripted_button = je_consolidate_afghanistan_resolve_southwest_conflict
	scripted_button = je_consolidate_afghanistan_resolve_northern_conflict

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				exists = c:AFG
			}
		}
		effect = {
			je_consolidate_afghanistan_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	on_weekly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = afghan_notification_sender
					scope:gp_scope = {
						has_variable = afghanistan_delimitation_treaty_approved
					}
					scope:rival_gp_scope = {
						has_variable = afghanistan_delimitation_treaty_approved
					}
					c:AFG ?= {
						NOT = {
							has_variable = received_afghan_border_notification
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { c:AFG ?= { has_variable = afg_proposal_ai_cooldown } }
					}
					c:AFG ?= {
						trigger_event = { id = gg_afghanistan.1 popup = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:AFG
			}
			c:AFG ?= {
				save_scope_as = afghanistan_scope
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = gp_scope
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_country = {
						has_variable = afghan_notification_sender
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = afghan_notification_sender
		}
		# Searches for the canonical GBR-RUS conference first
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:GBR ?= this
				exists = c:RUS
			}
			c:RUS ?= { save_temporary_scope_as = candidate_rival }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				c:RUS ?= this
				exists = c:GBR
			}
			c:GBR ?= { save_temporary_scope_as = candidate_rival }
		}

		# Priority: candidate GP itself > relevant candidate GP subject > any eligible GP > any eligible GP-subject
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:candidate_rival
				scope:candidate_rival ?= {
					country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
					c:AFG ?= {
						has_overlapping_interests = PREV # scope:candidate_rival
					}
					OR = {
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
							}
							any_neighbouring_state = { c:AFG ?= owner }
						}
						any_subject_or_below = {
							NOT = { c:AFG ?= this }
							any_scope_state = {
								OR = {
									is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
									is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
								}
								any_neighbouring_state = { c:AFG ?= owner }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			scope:candidate_rival = { save_scope_as = rival_gp_scope }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
					c:AFG ?= {
						has_overlapping_interests = PREV # scope:candidate_rival
					}
					OR = {
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
							}
							any_neighbouring_state = { c:AFG ?= owner }
						}
						any_subject_or_below = {
							NOT = { c:AFG ?= this }
							any_scope_state = {
								OR = {
									is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
									is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
								}
								any_neighbouring_state = { c:AFG ?= owner }
							}
						}
					}
					NOT = { THIS = ROOT }
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
					c:AFG ?= {
						has_overlapping_interests = PREV # scope:candidate_rival
					}
					OR = {
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
							}
							any_neighbouring_state = { c:AFG ?= owner }
						}
						any_subject_or_below = {
							NOT = { c:AFG ?= this }
							any_scope_state = {
								OR = {
									is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
									is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
								}
								any_neighbouring_state = { c:AFG ?= owner }
							}
						}
					}
					NOT = { THIS = ROOT }
				}
				save_scope_as = rival_gp_scope
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:rival_gp_scope ?=  {
					any_subject_or_below = {
						NOT = { c:AFG ?= this }
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
							}
							any_neighbouring_state = { c:AFG ?= owner }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			scope:rival_gp_scope ?=  {
				ordered_subject_or_below = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { c:AFG ?= this }
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
							}
							any_neighbouring_state = { c:AFG ?= owner }
						}
					}
					order_by = gdp
					position = 0
					check_range_bounds = no
					save_scope_as = rival_afghan_partition_proxy
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				any_subject_or_below = {
					NOT = {
						c:AFG ?= this
					}
					any_neighbouring_state = {
						c:AFG ?= owner
					}
				}
			}
			ordered_subject_or_below = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						c:AFG ?= this
					}
					any_neighbouring_state = {
						c:AFG ?= owner
					}
				}
				order_by = gdp
				position = 0
				check_range_bounds = no
				save_scope_as = afghan_partition_proxy
			}
		}
	}

	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = afghanistan_exists_tt
			exists = c:AFG
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = is_bordering_afghanistan_tt
			country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
			c:AFG ?= {
				has_overlapping_interests = ROOT
			}
			OR = {
				any_state_in_india = {
					OR = {
						owner ?= ROOT
						owner ?= { is_subject_of = ROOT }
					}
					any_neighbouring_state = {
						c:AFG ?= owner
					}
				}
				any_state_in_central_asia = {
					OR = {
						owner ?= ROOT
						owner ?= { is_subject_of = ROOT }
					}
					any_neighbouring_state = {
						c:AFG ?= owner
					}
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = another_gp_is_bordering_afghanistan_tt
			any_country = {
				country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
				NOT = {
					THIS = ROOT
				}
				c:AFG ?= {
					has_overlapping_interests = PREV # any_country
				}
				OR = {
					any_scope_state = {
						OR = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
						}
						any_neighbouring_state = {
							c:AFG ?= owner
						}
					}
					any_subject_or_below = {
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
							}
							any_neighbouring_state = {
								c:AFG ?= owner
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:gp_scope
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = first_gp_approves_tt
					scope:gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = afghanistan_delimitation_treaty_approved
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:rival_gp_scope
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = second_gp_approves_tt
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = afghanistan_delimitation_treaty_approved
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:afghanistan_scope
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = afghanistan_approves_tt
					c:AFG ?= {
						has_variable = afghanistan_delimitation_treaty_approved
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:AFG
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = afghanistan_abandon_claims_tt
					every_state_region = {
						limit = {
							c:AFG ?= {
								has_claim = prev
							}
						}
						remove_claim = c:AFG
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = proposed_durand_line
					}
				}
				c:AFG ?= {
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
							}
							set_state_owner = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						else = {
							set_state_owner = scope:rival_gp_scope
						}
					}
				}
				every_state_in_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							scope:rival_gp_scope ?= owner
							scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:AFG
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = proposed_indus_line
					}
				}
				c:AFG ?= {
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
							NOT = {
								state_region = {
									is_homeland = cu:pashtun
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
							}
							set_state_owner = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						else = {
							set_state_owner = scope:rival_gp_scope
						}
					}
				}
				every_state_in_greater_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							scope:rival_gp_scope ?= owner
							scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:AFG
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = proposed_status_quo
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = status_quo_southeast_tt
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = proposed_torkestan_line
					}
				}
				c:AFG ?= {
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
								state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
							}
							set_state_owner = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						else = {
							set_state_owner = scope:rival_gp_scope
						}
					}
				}
				every_state_in_greater_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							scope:rival_gp_scope ?= owner
							scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
						}
						NOT = {
							state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:AFG
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = proposed_amu_darya_line
					}
				}
				c:AFG ?= {
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
							}
							set_state_owner = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						else = {
							set_state_owner = scope:rival_gp_scope
						}
					}
				}
				every_state_in_greater_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							scope:rival_gp_scope ?= owner
							scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:AFG
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = proposed_status_quo_north
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = status_quo_north_tt
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = proposed_compensation_for_afghanistan
					}
				}
				scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
					create_treaty = {
						first_country = scope:rival_gp_scope
						second_country = c:AFG

						is_draft = no
						binding_period = { years = 2 }

						articles_to_create = {
							{
								article = money_transfer
								source_country = scope:rival_gp_scope
								target_country = c:AFG
								inputs = {
									{ quantity = 1000 }
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_variable = proposed_durand_line
					}
				}
				c:AFG ?= {
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
							}
							set_state_owner = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						else = {
							set_state_owner = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				every_state_in_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							ROOT ?= owner
							scope:afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:AFG
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_variable = proposed_indus_line
					}
				}
				c:AFG ?= {
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
							NOT = {
								state_region = {
									is_homeland = cu:pashtun
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
							}
							set_state_owner = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						else = {
							set_state_owner = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				every_state_in_greater_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							ROOT ?= owner
							scope:afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:AFG
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_variable = proposed_status_quo
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = status_quo_southeast_tt
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = proposed_torkestan_line
				}
				c:AFG ?= {
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
								state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
							}
							set_state_owner = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						else = {
							set_state_owner = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				every_state_in_greater_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							ROOT ?= owner
							scope:afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
						}
						NOT = {
							state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:AFG
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_variable = proposed_amu_darya_line
					}
				}
				c:AFG ?= {
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_asia
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
							}
							set_state_owner = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						else = {
							set_state_owner = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				every_state_in_greater_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							ROOT ?= owner
							scope:afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:AFG
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_variable = proposed_status_quo_north
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = status_quo_north_tt
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_variable = proposed_compensation_for_afghanistan
					}
				}
				create_treaty = {
					first_country = root
					second_country = c:AFG

					is_draft = no
					binding_period = { years = 2 }

					articles_to_create = {
						{
							article = money_transfer
							source_country = root
							target_country = c:AFG
							inputs = {
								{ quantity = 1000 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_global_variable = afghan_borders_solidified
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:RUS ?= this
				}
				set_global_variable = afghanistan_russia_victory_var
				great_game_russia_progress_10 = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					c:GBR ?= this
				}
				set_global_variable = afghanistan_britain_victory_var
				great_game_britain_progress_10 = yes
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				clear_afghanistan_variables = yes
			}
			je_consolidate_afghanistan_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	timeout = 365

	outcome = {
		header = JOURNAL_ENTRY_TIMEOUT
		effect_header = JOURNAL_ENTRY_ON_TIMEOUT
		flags = { timeout }
		conclusion_desc = JOURNAL_ENTRY_TIMEOUT_CONCLUSION_DESC
		effect = {
			custom_tooltip = negotiations_failure_tt
			# Southeast - rival_gp_scope
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = proposed_durand_line
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_india = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							c:AFG ?= owner
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						add_claim = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
					}
					else = {
						add_claim = scope:rival_gp_scope
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							any_scope_state = {
								has_claim_by = c:AFG
							}
						}
						NOT = { s:STATE_HERAT ?= this }
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								scope:rival_gp_scope ?= owner
								scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
							}
						}
					}
					add_claim = c:AFG
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = proposed_indus_line
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_india = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { is_homeland = cu:pashtun }
						any_scope_state = {
							c:AFG ?= owner
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						add_claim = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
					}
					else = {
						add_claim = scope:rival_gp_scope
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_greater_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							any_scope_state = {
								has_claim_by = c:AFG
							}
						}
						NOT = { s:STATE_HERAT ?= this }
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								scope:rival_gp_scope ?= owner
								scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
							}
						}
					}
					add_claim = c:AFG
				}
			}
			# North - rival_gp_scope
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = proposed_torkestan_line
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_central_asia = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							c:AFG ?= owner
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						add_claim = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
					}
					else = {
						add_claim = scope:rival_gp_scope
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						c:AFG ?= {
							has_state_in_state_region = STATE_HERAT
						}
					}
					s:STATE_HERAT ?= {
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
							}
							add_claim = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						else = {
							add_claim = scope:rival_gp_scope
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							s:STATE_HERAT ?= {
								any_scope_state = {
									has_claim_by = c:AFG
								}
							}
						}
						OR = {
							scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy ?= {
								has_state_in_state_region = STATE_HERAT
							}
							scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
								has_state_in_state_region = STATE_HERAT
							}
						}
					}
					s:STATE_HERAT ?= {
						add_claim = c:AFG
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:rival_gp_scope ?= {
						has_variable = proposed_amu_darya_line
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_central_asia = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							c:AFG ?= owner
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						add_claim = scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy
					}
					else = {
						add_claim = scope:rival_gp_scope
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							any_scope_state = {
								has_claim_by = c:AFG
							}
						}
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								scope:rival_gp_scope ?= owner
								scope:rival_afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
							}
						}
					}
					add_claim = c:AFG
				}
			}
			# Southeast - ROOT
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_variable = proposed_durand_line
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_india = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							c:AFG ?= owner
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						add_claim = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
					}
					else = {
						add_claim = ROOT
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							any_scope_state = {
								has_claim_by = c:AFG
							}
						}
						NOT = { s:STATE_HERAT ?= this }
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								ROOT ?= owner
								scope:afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
							}
						}
					}
					add_claim = c:AFG
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_variable = proposed_indus_line
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_india = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { is_homeland = cu:pashtun }
						any_scope_state = {
							c:AFG ?= owner
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						add_claim = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
					}
					else = {
						add_claim = ROOT
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_greater_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							any_scope_state = {
								has_claim_by = c:AFG
							}
						}
						NOT = { s:STATE_HERAT ?= this }
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								ROOT ?= owner
								scope:afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
							}
						}
					}
					add_claim = c:AFG
				}
			}

			# North - ROOT
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_variable = proposed_torkestan_line
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_central_asia = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							c:AFG ?= owner
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						add_claim = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
					}
					else = {
						add_claim = ROOT
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						c:AFG ?= {
							has_state_in_state_region = STATE_HERAT
						}
					}
					s:STATE_HERAT ?= {
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
							}
							add_claim = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						else = {
							add_claim = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							s:STATE_HERAT ?= {
								any_scope_state = {
									has_claim_by = c:AFG
								}
							}
						}
						OR = {
							scope:afghan_partition_proxy ?= {
								has_state_in_state_region = STATE_HERAT
							}
							ROOT = {
								has_state_in_state_region = STATE_HERAT
							}
						}
					}
					s:STATE_HERAT ?= {
						add_claim = c:AFG
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_variable = proposed_amu_darya_line
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_central_asia = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_state = {
							c:AFG ?= owner
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
						}
						add_claim = scope:afghan_partition_proxy
					}
					else = {
						add_claim = ROOT
					}
				}
				every_state_region_in_afghanistan = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							any_scope_state = {
								has_claim_by = c:AFG
							}
						}
						any_scope_state = {
							OR = {
								ROOT ?= owner
								scope:afghan_partition_proxy ?= owner
							}
						}
					}
					add_claim = c:AFG
				}
			}

			hidden_effect = {
				clear_afghanistan_variables = yes
			}
			je_consolidate_afghanistan_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	weight = 1001
}

REPLACE:je_caucasian_war = {

    }

                    is_split_state = no
                }

                                any_state_in_caucasus_old = {
                                    state_region = s:STATE_GREATER_CAUCASUS
                                    is_in_same_market_area = scope:russia_scope.capital
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                NOT = {
                    exists = scope:russia_scope
                }
            }
        }
        effect = {
            set_global_variable = caucasus_failure_var
            scope:russia_scope ?= {
                trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar_end.4 days = 0 }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        is_involved_in_journal_entry = je_great_game_control
                    }
                    great_game_britain_progress_15 = yes
                }
                show_as_tooltip = {
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            has_journal_entry = je_tsarpoints_master
                        }
                        je_tsarpoints_subtract_med = yes
                    }
                }
            }
            scope:circassia_scope ?= {
                trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar_end.2 }
            }
            scope:imamate_scope ?= {
                trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar_end.5 }
            }
            hidden_effect = {
                every_state_in_caucasus_old = {
                    remove_modifier = caucasus_war_attacker
                }
            }
        }
        event_outcome_desc = {
            first_valid = {
                triggered_desc = {
                    desc = event_effects_caucasuswar_end.4_tt
                }
            }
        }
    }

    on_weekly_pulse = {
        effect = {
            scope:russia_scope ?= {
                trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar_end.1 }
                trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar_end.3 }
            }
        }
    }

    outcome = {
        flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
        conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
        trigger = {
            OR = {
                NOT = { exists = c:RUS }
                c:CIR ?= {
                    is_subject = yes
                    NOT = { is_at_war_with_overlord = yes }
                    NOT = {
                        is_subject_of = c:RUS
                    }
                }
                c:CHC ?= {
                    is_subject = yes
                    NOT = { is_at_war_with_overlord = yes }
                    NOT = {
                        is_subject_of = c:RUS
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        effect = {
            set_global_variable = caucasus_failure_var
            c:RUS ?= {
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        is_involved_in_journal_entry = je_great_game_control
                    }
                    great_game_britain_progress_15 = yes
                }
            }
            scope:circassia_scope ?= {
                remove_modifier = caucasus_war_defender_circassia
            }
            scope:imamate_scope ?= {
                remove_modifier = caucasus_war_defender_imamate
            }
            je_caucasian_war_cleanup = yes
        }
    }

    on_monthly_pulse = {
        effect = {
            if = {
                limit = {
                    scope:russia_scope ?= {
                        NOT = {
                            any_state_in_caucasus_old = {
                                state_region = s:STATE_GREATER_CAUCASUS
                                is_in_same_market_area = scope:russia_scope.capital
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                scope:journal_entry ?= {
                    change_variable = {
                        name = caucasus_doom_var
                        add = 1
                    }
                }
            }
            else = {
                scope:journal_entry ?= {
                    set_variable = { name = caucasus_doom_var value = 0 }
                }
            }
            scope:circassia_scope ?= {
                random_list = {
                    100 = {
                    }
                    30 = {
                        trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar.7 }
                    }
                    10 = {
                        trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar.10 }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    on_yearly_pulse = {
        effect = {
            scope:russia_scope ?= {
                random_list = {
                    90 = {
                    }
                    10 = {
                        trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar.1 }
                    }
                    10 = {
                        trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar.2 }
                    }
                    10 = {
                        trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar.3 }
                    }
                    10 = {
                        trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar.4 }
                    }
                    50 = {
                        trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar.6 }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    weight = 100

    should_be_pinned_by_default_involved = yes
    should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = no

    can_revolution_inherit = yes
}

REPLACE:je_circassian_expulsions = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

    group = je_group_russia_expansion

    activation_description = {
        always = yes
    }

    is_shown_when_inactive = {
        has_variable = milyutin_memorandum_accepted
    }

    possible = {
        custom_tooltip = {
            text = circassia_defeated_tt
            NOT = {
                exists = c:CIR
            }
        }
        cultural_acceptance_base = { target = cu:circassian value < acceptance_status_4 }
        owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KUBAN
    }

    immediate = {
        cu:circassian = {
            save_scope_as = circassian_culture_scope
        }
        random_country = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
                    is_incorporated = yes
					OR = {
						state_region = s:STATE_EASTERN_THRACE
						state_region = s:STATE_TRABZON
						state_region = s:STATE_TRABZON
					}
                    NOT = { owner = ROOT }
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = expulsion_destination_country
            trigger_event = { id = caucasus_expulsions.2 days = 3 popup = yes }
		}
    }

    scripted_button = russia_halt_circassian_expulsions

    outcome = {
        header = je_standard_completion_header
        effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
        color = "gold_positive"
        conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
        trigger = {
            any_state_in_caucasus_old = {
                filter = {
                    owner = ROOT
                }
                custom_tooltip = {
                    text = circassians_below_5_percent
                    culture_percent_state = {
                        target = cu:circassian
                        value <= 0.05
                    }
                }
                percent = 1
            }
        }
        effect = {
            if = {
                limit = {
                    has_journal_entry = je_tsarpoints_master
                }
                je_tsarpoints_add_low = yes
            }
            je_circassian_expulsions_cleanup = yes
        }
    }

    outcome = {
        header = je_standard_failure_header
        effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
        color = "blue_negative"
        conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
        trigger = {
           OR = {
                custom_tooltip = {
                    text = has_halted_deportations_tt
                    has_variable = halted_circassian_deportations
                }
                c:RUS ?= {
                    cultural_acceptance_base = { target = cu:circassian value >= acceptance_status_4 }
                }
            }
        }
        effect = {
            je_circassian_expulsions_cleanup = yes
        }
    }

    on_monthly_pulse = {
        events = {
            caucasus_expulsions.1
        }
        effect = {
            # future deportation events are hidden, effects selected in caucasus_expulsions.1 options apply automatically
            if = { # caucasus_expulsions.1.a
                limit = {
                    has_variable = circassian_events_hidden_a
                    circassian_expulsion_destination_available_trigger = yes
                    NOT = {
                        has_variable = circassian_expulsion_stall
                    }
                }
                random_scope_state = {
                    limit = {
                        is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_caucasus_old
                        any_scope_pop = {
                            culture = cu:circassian
                        }
                    }
                    save_scope_as = circassian_expulsion_state
                }
                circassian_expulsion_pick_destination_effect = yes # this sets expulsion_destination_country and expulsion_destination_state
                cu:circassian = {
                    save_scope_as = circassian_culture
                }
                set_variable = {
                    name = circassian_expulsion_stall
                    months = 3
                }
                scope:circassian_expulsion_state = {
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:expulsion_destination_country = {
                                has_variable = refused_circassian_refugees
                            }
                        }
                        kill_population_percent_in_state = {
                            percent = 0.06
                            culture = cu:circassian
                        }
                    }
                    else = {
                        kill_population_percent_in_state = {
                            percent = 0.04
                            culture = cu:circassian
                        }
                    }
                    custom_tooltip = {
                        text = three_pops_deported_tt
                        random_scope_pop = {
                            limit = {
                                culture = cu:circassian
                                strata = lower
                            }
                            move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
                        }
                        random_scope_pop = {
                            limit = {
                                culture = cu:circassian
                                strata = middle
                            }
                            move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
                        }
                        random_scope_pop = {
                            limit = {
                                culture = cu:circassian
                                strata = upper
                            }
                            move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
                        }
                    }
                }
                scope:expulsion_destination_country = {
                    post_notification = ottoman_circassian_refugees_arrived
                }
                post_notification = circassians_deported_message
            }
            else_if = { # caucasus_expulsions.1.b
                limit = {
                    has_variable = circassian_events_hidden_b
                    circassian_expulsion_destination_available_trigger = yes
                    NOT = {
                        has_variable = circassian_expulsion_stall
                    }
                }
                random_scope_state = {
                    limit = {
                        is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_caucasus_old
                        any_scope_pop = {
                            culture = cu:circassian
                        }
                    }
                    save_scope_as = circassian_expulsion_state
                }
                circassian_expulsion_pick_destination_effect = yes # this sets expulsion_destination_country and expulsion_destination_state
                cu:circassian = {
                    save_scope_as = circassian_culture
                }
                set_variable = {
                    name = circassian_expulsion_stall
                    months = 3
                }
                scope:circassian_expulsion_state = {
                    kill_population_percent_in_state = {
                        percent = 0.01
                        culture = cu:circassian
                    }
                    custom_tooltip = {
                        text = three_pops_deported_tt
                        random_scope_pop = {
                            limit = {
                                culture = cu:circassian
                                OR = {
                                    strata = lower
                                    strata = middle
                                }
                            }
                            move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
                        }
                        random_scope_pop = {
                            limit = {
                                culture = cu:circassian
                                OR = {
                                    strata = middle
                                    strata = upper
                                }
                            }
                            move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
                        }
                    }
                }
                scope:expulsion_destination_country = {
                    post_notification = ottoman_circassian_refugees_arrived
                }
                post_notification = circassians_deported_message
            }

            random_list = {
                90 = {

                }
                10 = {
                    random_country = {
                        limit = {
                            NOT = {
                                this = root
                            }
                            any_scope_state = {
                                OR = {
                                    state_region = s:STATE_EASTERN_THRACE
                                    state_region = s:STATE_TRABZON
                                    state_region = s:STATE_TRABZON
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        trigger_event = { id = caucasus_expulsions.3 popup = yes }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    weight = 100

    	
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}

                        je_tsarpoints_add_low = yes
                    }
                }
            }
        }

            first_valid = {

                }
            }
        }
    }

REPLACE:je_conquest_of_central_asia = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

    group = je_group_russia_expansion
    dlc = dlc010

    activation_description = {
        always = yes
    }

    scripted_button = je_conquest_of_central_asia_turkmen_button

    is_shown_when_inactive = {
        OR = {
            has_dlc_feature = ep1_content # Sphere of Influence
            has_dlc_feature = ip5_content # State and Revolution
        }
        exists = c:RUS
        c:RUS ?= THIS
        is_independent_and_not_being_subjugated = yes
        any_scope_state = {
            state_region = s:STATE_URALSK
        }
        any_scope_state = {
            state_region = s:STATE_AKMOLINSK
        }
        owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_URALSK
        owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_AKMOLINSK
        owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_SEMIRECHE
    }
    possible = {
        has_technology_researched = civilizing_mission
        any_scope_state = {
            state_region = s:STATE_URALSK
        }
        any_scope_state = {
            state_region = s:STATE_AKMOLINSK
        }
        owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_URALSK
        owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_AKMOLINSK
        owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_SEMIRECHE
    }
    immediate = {
        trigger_event = { id = russian_central_asia.1 } # Central Asia initialisation
        set_global_variable = central_asia_consolidation_happened
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = c:TRM
            }
            c:TRM ?= {
                save_scope_as = turkmenia_scope
            }
        }
        else = {
            random_neighbouring_state = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        state_region = s:STATE_KHIVA
                        state_region = s:STATE_AKMOLINSK
                        state_region = s:STATE_UZBEKIA
                        state_region = s:STATE_KIRGHIZIA
                        state_region = s:STATE_KHIVA
                        state_region = s:STATE_TAJIKISTAN
                    }
                    not = { owner = ROOT }
                }
                owner = {
                    save_scope_as = turkmenia_scope
                }
            }
        }
    }

    outcome = {
        header = je_standard_completion_header
        effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
        color = "gold_positive"
        conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
        trigger = {
            country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_AKMOLINSK
            country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_UZBEKIA
            country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KIRGHIZIA
            country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region_uncontested = STATE_KHIVA # Chorasmia

REPLACE:je_uneasy_raj = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	group = je_group_global_international_situations

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	should_be_involved = {
		c:BIC ?= this
	}

	should_show_when_not_involved = {
		OR = {
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
			any_subject_or_below = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
			}
		}
	}

	scripted_progress_bar = sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bengal
	scripted_progress_bar = sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bombay
	scripted_progress_bar = sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_madras

	immediate = {
		c:BIC ?= {
			save_scope_as = raj_scope
			set_global_variable = { name = bengal_presidency_stability value = "scope:journal_entry.scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bengal)" }
			set_global_variable = { name = bombay_presidency_stability value = "scope:journal_entry.scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bombay)" }
			set_global_variable = { name = madras_presidency_stability value = "scope:journal_entry.scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_madras)" }
			set_variable = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_timer_var
				value = 0
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_uneasy_raj_complete_header
		effect_header = je_uneasy_raj_on_complete_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_uneasy_raj_complete_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			c:BIC ?= {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_avert_sepoy_mutiny_tt_1
					has_variable = consolidated_colonial_rule
				}
				average_sol >= 10
				radical_fraction = {
					value <= 0.10
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			c:BIC ?= {
				custom_tooltip = home_rule_unavailable_tt
				custom_tooltip = end_company_rule_made_available_tt
				trigger_event = { id = sepoy_mutiny_events.11 popup = yes }
				remove_global_variable = bengal_presidency_stability
				remove_global_variable = bombay_presidency_stability
				remove_global_variable = madras_presidency_stability
				set_variable = company_rule_enduring
			}
		}
		event_outcome_desc = {
			first_valid = {
				triggered_desc = {
					desc = event_effects_sepoy_mutiny_events.11_tt
				}
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_uneasy_raj_fail_header
		effect_header = je_uneasy_raj_on_fail_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_uneasy_raj_fail_conclusion_desc
		flags = { historical }
		trigger = {
			c:BIC ?= {
				OR = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = je_uneasy_raj_tt_2
						var:sepoy_mutiny_timer_var >= 100
					}
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = time_has_run_out_tt
						NOT = {
							has_journal_entry = je_uneasy_raj
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			c:BIC ?= {
				custom_tooltip = british_raj_later_tt
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_variable = utilitarianism_done
						}
					}
					custom_tooltip = no_more_utilitarianism_tt
				}
				add_journal_entry = { type = je_sepoy_mutiny }
				trigger_event = { id = sepoy_mutiny_events.3 popup = yes }
				# Bengal Presidency
				if = {
					limit = {
						je:je_uneasy_raj ?= {
							"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bengal)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_low_lower_bound
						}
					}
					set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_bengal_effect_critical
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						je:je_uneasy_raj ?= {
							"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bengal)" > uneasy_raj_stability_low_lower_bound
							"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bengal)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_medium_lower_bound
						}
					}
					set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_bengal_effect_high
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						je:je_uneasy_raj ?= {
							"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bengal)" > uneasy_raj_stability_medium_lower_bound
							"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bengal)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_high_lower_bound
						}
					}
					set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_bengal_effect_medium
				}
				if = {

			c:BIC ?= {
				set_global_variable = { name = bengal_presidency_stability value = "scope:journal_entry.scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bengal)" }
				set_global_variable = { name = bombay_presidency_stability value = "scope:journal_entry.scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bombay)" }
				set_global_variable = { name = madras_presidency_stability value = "scope:journal_entry.scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_madras)" }
			}
		}
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:BIC ?= {
						OR = {
							je:je_uneasy_raj ?= {
								"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bengal)" < uneasy_raj_stability_medium_upper_bound
							}
							je:je_uneasy_raj ?= {
								"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bombay)" < uneasy_raj_stability_medium_upper_bound
							}
							je:je_uneasy_raj ?= {
								"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_madras)" < uneasy_raj_stability_medium_upper_bound
							}
						}
					}
				}
				c:BIC ?= {
					change_variable = {
						name = sepoy_mutiny_timer_var
						add = 1
					}
				}
			}
			c:BIC ?= {
				random_list = {
					11 = { }
					1 = { trigger_event = sepoy_mutiny_events.6 }
					1 = { trigger_event = raj.4 }
					1 = { trigger_event = raj.5 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	weight = 100

	should_be_pinned_by_default_involved = yes
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = no

	can_revolution_inherit = yes
}

REPLACE:je_sepoy_mutiny = {

	}

		}

		header = je_standard_completion_header

		color = "gold_positive"

		trigger = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					c:MUG ?= {
						has_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					}
				}
				c:MUG ?= { is_country_alive = no }
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					c:BGL ?= {
						has_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					}
				}
				c:BGL ?= { is_country_alive = no }
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					c:SAT ?= {
						has_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					}
				}
				c:SAT ?= { is_country_alive = no }
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					c:PAN ?= {
						has_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					}
				}
				c:PAN ?= { is_country_alive = no }
			}
			highest_secession_progress < 0.3
			has_any_secessionists_broken_out = no
			is_at_war = no
		}
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:GBR
					is_subject_of = c:GBR
					c:GBR ?= {
						is_country_alive = yes
						NOT = { has_variable = british_raj_var }
					}
				}
				c:GBR ?= {
					trigger_event = { id = raj.1 days = 0 popup = yes }
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = company_crushed_mutiny
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						owner = ROOT
						has_variable = bic_incorporated_state
					}
					set_state_type = incorporated
					remove_variable = bic_incorporated_state
				}
			}
			je_sepoy_mutiny_cleanup = yes
		}
		event_outcome_desc = {
			first_valid = {
            triggered_desc = {
                desc = event_effects_raj.1_tt
            }
        }
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
		OR = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					c:MUG ?= {
						has_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					}
				}
				exists = c:MUG
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = c:MUG
					has_state_in_state_region = STATE_DELHI
					has_state_in_state_region = STATE_AGRA
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					c:BGL ?= {
						has_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					}
				}
				exists = c:BGL
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = c:BGL
					has_state_in_state_region = STATE_EAST_BENGAL
					has_state_in_state_region = STATE_WEST_BENGAL
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {

				exists = c:SAT
				NOT = {

				limit = {
					c:PAN ?= {
						has_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					}

				exists = c:PAN
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = c:PAN

					has_state_in_state_region = STATE_DELHI
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					c:KNO ?= {
						has_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					}
				}
				exists = c:KNO
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = c:KNO
					has_state_in_state_region = STATE_MADRAS
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_trigger = {

					any_scope_play_involved = { this = root }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny

REPLACE:je_matter_of_hungary = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/war_goals/unify_italy.dds"

	group = je_group_historical_content

	activation_description = {
		always = yes
	}

	scripted_button = matter_of_hungary_dual_monarchy_button
	scripted_button = matter_of_hungary_integrate_crown_lands_button

	is_shown_in_lobby = {
		c:AUS ?= this
	}

	immediate = {
		cu:hungarian = {
			save_scope_as = hungarian_scope
		}
		c:HUN ?= { save_scope_as = hungary_scope }
		set_variable = { name = hungarian_acceptance_var value = 0 }
	}

	modifiers_while_active = {
		modifier_young_hungary_movement
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_average_culture_pop_acceptance = {
						culture = cu:hungarian
						value >= 80
					}
				}
				change_variable = { name = hungarian_acceptance_var add = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { name = hungarian_acceptance_var value = 0 }
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_matter_of_hungary_completion_autonomy_tt
					has_variable = established_austria_hungary_var
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = hungarian_above_80_acceptance_for_a_while_tt
					has_variable = hungarian_acceptance_var
					var:hungarian_acceptance_var >= 60 # This 60 has nothing to do with the acceptance value itself
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = {
						exists = c:HUN
					}
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = hungarian_movements_pacified_tt
						any_political_movement = {
							is_political_movement_type = movement_cultural_minority
							culture ?= cu:hungarian
							political_movement_radicalism < define:NPolitics|RADICALISM_AGITATING_THRESHOLD
						}
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = {
							is_homeland = cu:hungarian
						}
						count >= 8
						is_incorporated = yes
						turmoil < 0.05
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			scope:journal_entry = {
			remove_modifier = modifier_young_hungary_movement
		}
			je_matter_of_hungary_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = je_matter_of_hungary_fail_hungary_is_free
				OR = {
					AND = {
						NOT = {
							any_scope_state = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_hungary
							}
						}
						c:HUN ?= {
							NOT = {
								is_subject_of = root
							}
						}
					}
					c:HUN ?= {
						NOT = {
							is_subject_of = root
						}
					}
				}
			}
			c:HUN ?= {
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						state_region = s:STATE_SLOVAKIA
						state_region = s:STATE_CENTRAL_HUNGARY
						state_region = s:STATE_CENTRAL_HUNGARY
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = je_matter_of_hungary_fail_not_at_war_with_hungary
					c:HUN ?= {
					NOR = {
						is_secessionist = yes
						is_revolutionary = yes
						is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = root
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = austria_events.3 popup = yes }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = je_matter_of_hungary_on_fail_lose_control
			}
		}
		event_outcome_desc = {
			first_valid = {
				triggered_desc = {
					desc = event_effects_austria_events.3_tt
				}
			}
		}
	}

	weight = 100

	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}

REPLACE:je_austrian_neo_absolutism = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

		always = yes
	}

	}

	possible = {
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy

					OR = {
						is_subject_type = subject_type_crown_land
						is_subject_type = subject_type_personal_union
					}
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			any_interest_group_in_government = {
				is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
				is_powerful = yes
			}

				text = pb_ruler_tt
				ruler.interest_group ?= ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {

			name = austrian_gdp_var
			value = root.gdp
		}
	}

	modifiers_while_active = {
		modifier_austrian_state_police
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			calc_true_if = {
				amount >= 4
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = austrian_gdp_var
					}
					gdp > {
						value = var:austrian_gdp_var
						multiply = 1.5
					}
				}
				AND = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = austrian_rank_three_or_above_tt
						global_country_ranking <= 3
					}
					OR = {
						NAND = {
							exists = c:PRU
							c:GER ?= {
								NOT = {
									was_formed_from = AUS
								}
							}
						}
						trigger_if = {
							limit = {
								exists = c:PRU
							}
							custom_tooltip = {
								text = austrian_gdp_higher_than_prussia
								root.gdp > c:PRU.gdp
							}
							custom_tooltip = {
								text = enforced_wargoal_on_prussia_tt
								has_variable = has_enforced_wargoal_on_prussia_var
							}
						}
						trigger_if = {
							limit = {
								exists = c:GER
								c:GER ?= {
									NOT = {
										was_formed_from = AUS
									}
								}
							}
							custom_tooltip = {
								text = austrian_gdp_higher_than_germany
								root.gdp > c:GER.gdp
							}
							custom_tooltip = {
								text = enforced_wargoal_on_germany_tt
								has_variable = has_enforced_wargoal_on_germany_var
							}
						}
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_completion_crown_lands_tt
					has_variable = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_marginalized_crown_diets_var
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_completion_papal_concordat_tt
					has_variable = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_marginalized_papal_concordat_var
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_completion_feudal_obligations_tt
					has_variable = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_marginalized_feudal_obligations_var
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			show_as_tooltip = {
				activate_law = law_type:law_neo_absolutism
			}
			trigger_event = { id = austria_events.1 popup = yes }
		}
		event_outcome_desc = {
			first_valid = {
				triggered_desc = {
					desc = event_effects_austria_events.1_tt
				}
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_fail_tt
					je:je_austrian_neo_absolutism ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(austrian_neo_absolutism_constitutional_pressure_progress_bar)" >= 100
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_hungary_subject_tt
					has_variable = cisleithania_var
					any_subject_or_below = {
						has_variable = transleithania_var
						is_subject_type = subject_type_personal_union
					}

				NOT = {

				}
				NOT = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_secret_police
				}
			}

		effect = {

				limit = {
					je:je_austrian_neo_absolutism ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(austrian_neo_absolutism_constitutional_pressure_progress_bar)" >= 100
					}
				}

			}
			else = {
				trigger_event = { id = austria_events.5 popup = yes }
			}

			je_austrian_neo_absolutism_cleanup = yes
		}
		event_outcome_desc = {

					trigger = {

							"scripted_bar_progress(austrian_neo_absolutism_constitutional_pressure_progress_bar)" >= 100
						}

					}
					desc = event_effects_austria_events.5_tt
				}
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {

			OR = {

				}
				NOT = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_secret_police
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			set_variable = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_failed_var
			je_austrian_neo_absolutism_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	status_desc = {

				desc = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_status

					}
				}
			}

					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_status_2

					scope:journal_entry ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(austrian_neo_absolutism_constitutional_pressure_progress_bar)" > 25
					}

						"scripted_bar_progress(austrian_neo_absolutism_constitutional_pressure_progress_bar)" <= 50
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_status_3
				trigger = {
					scope:journal_entry ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(austrian_neo_absolutism_constitutional_pressure_progress_bar)" > 50
					}
					scope:journal_entry ?= {
						"scripted_bar_progress(austrian_neo_absolutism_constitutional_pressure_progress_bar)" <= 75
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = je_austrian_neo_absolutism_status_4
				trigger = {

						"scripted_bar_progress(austrian_neo_absolutism_constitutional_pressure_progress_bar)" > 75
					}

	scripted_button = je_danubian_federation_serb_button
	scripted_button = je_danubian_federation_ukrainian_button
	scripted_button = je_danubian_federation_enact_the_federation

	on_weekly_pulse = {

	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			danubian_federation_calculate_path_to_victory = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = count_integrated_cultures
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = has_integrated_5_danubian_cultures_tt
					var:count_integrated_cultures >= 5
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = danubian_federation_final_act_tt
				has_variable = danubian_federation_final_act
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					any_subject_or_below = {
						OR = {
							is_subject_type = subject_type_personal_union
							is_subject_type = subject_type_crown_land
							is_subject_type = subject_type_puppet
						}
						capital = {
							OR = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_danubia_old
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_poland_old
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_balkans_old
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_south_germany_old

								state_region = s:STATE_CROATIA
								state_region = s:STATE_VENETIA
							}
						}
					}
				}
				any_subject_or_below = {
					filter = {
						OR = {
							is_subject_type = subject_type_personal_union
							is_subject_type = subject_type_crown_land
							is_subject_type = subject_type_puppet
						}
						capital = {
							OR = {
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_danubia_old
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_poland_old
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_balkans_old
								is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_south_germany_old
								state_region = s:STATE_CROATIA
								state_region = s:STATE_VENETIA
							}
						}
					}
					custom_tooltip = capital_is_in_danubian_states_tt
					OR = {
						custom_tooltip = {
							text = all_primary_cultures_integrated_tt
							any_primary_culture = {
								save_temporary_scope_as = subject_primary_culture
								ROOT = {
									any_in_list = {
										variable = aus_integrated_cultures
										scope:subject_primary_culture ?= this
									}
								}
								percent = 1
							}
						}
						AND = {
							relations:root >= relations_threshold:amicable
							liberty_desire < define:NDiplomacy|WAR_GOAL_INFAMY_LOW_LIBERTY_DESIRE_THRESHOLD
						}
					}
					count = all
				}
			}
			is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = no
			is_at_war = no
		}
		effect = {
			every_subject_or_below = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_subject_type = subject_type_personal_union
						is_subject_type = subject_type_crown_land
						is_subject_type = subject_type_puppet
					}
					capital = {
						OR = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_danubia_old
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_poland_old
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_balkans_old
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_south_germany_old
							state_region = s:STATE_CROATIA
							state_region = s:STATE_VENETIA
						}
					}
				}
				c:AUS ?= {
					annex_with_incorporation = prev
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = danubian_tradition_traits_tt
				every_scope_culture = {
					limit = {
						has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european
						save_temporary_scope_as = temp_culture_scope
						ROOT = {
							any_scope_state = {
								OR = {
									is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_danubia_old
									is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_poland_old
									is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_balkans_old
									is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_south_germany_old
									state_region = s:STATE_CROATIA
									state_region = s:STATE_VENETIA
								}
								state_region = {
									is_homeland = scope:temp_culture_scope
								}
							}
						}
					}
					add_tradition_trait = tradition_danubian
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = change_tag_to_KUK_monarchy_tt
					change_tag = KUK
				}
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = change_tag_to_KUK_republic_tt
					change_tag = KUK
				}
			}
			trigger_event = { id = austria_federation.2 }
			set_variable = danubian_federation_done_with
			set_variable = achievement_danubian_federation
		}
		event_outcome_desc = {
			first_valid = {
				triggered_desc = {
					desc = event_effects_austria_federation.2_tt
				}
			}
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_failure_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_failure_header
		color = "blue_negative"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = danubian_federation_potential_points
						danubian_federation_remaining_cultures_to_integrate > 0
					}
					custom_tooltip = { # failsafe
						text = has_no_way_to_integrate_5_danubian_cultures_tt
						AND = {
							var:count_integrated_cultures < 5
							var:danubian_federation_potential_points <= {
								value = 5 # The minimum number of cultures needed to get over the hill
								subtract = var:count_integrated_cultures
							}
						}
					}
				}
				any_civil_war = {
					civil_war_progress >= 0.75
					OR = {
						is_civil_war_type = revolution
						AND = {
							is_civil_war_type = secession
							political_movement.culture ?= {
								OR = {
									has_discrimination_trait_group = language_group_slavic
									has_discrimination_trait = heritage_romanian
									has_discrimination_trait = heritage_magyar
									has_discrimination_trait = heritage_italic
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_ethnostate
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_national_supremacy
			}
		}
		effect = {
			custom_tooltip = lose_integrated_cultures_tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable_list = aus_integrated_cultures
				}
				every_in_list = {
					variable = aus_integrated_cultures
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_primary_culture_of = ROOT
						}
						ROOT = {
							remove_primary_culture = prev
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = danubian_federation_done_with
			je_danubian_federation_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		flags = { hidden invalid_outcome }
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_failure_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			has_variable = shelved_federation_proposal
		}
		effect = {
			je_danubian_federation_cleanup = yes
		}
	}

	weight = 100

		
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}

REPLACE:je_eastern_question_russia = {
	group = je_group_foreign_affairs
	dlc = dlc014

		always = yes
	}

		has_dlc_feature = ip3_content
		OR = {
			c:RUS ?= this
			c:UKR ?= this
		}
		NOT = {
			exists = c:BUL
		}
		c:TUR ?= {
			has_strategic_adjacency = ROOT
		}
	}

	possible = {
		c:TUR ?= {
			has_journal_entry = je_great_eastern_crisis
		}
	}

	modifiers_while_active = {

	}

	immediate = {

		c:TUR ?= {
			save_scope_as = ottoman_scope
		}

			save_scope_as = root_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:TUR ?= {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_GREATER_CAUCASUS
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = ottomans_own_kars_at_start
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:TUR ?= {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_WALLACHIA
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = ottomans_own_dobrudja_at_start
		}
	}

	outcome = {
		header = je_standard_completion_header
		effect_header = je_standard_on_completion_header
		color = "gold_positive"
		conclusion_desc = je_standard_completion_conclusion_desc
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = bulgaria_is_independent_tt
				any_country_in_balkans = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:bulgarian
